ici eṉṉum col icivippatu- taṉ vayappaṭuttuvatu eṉap poruḷpaṭum. ici kal maṉattiyum karinturukac ceyyum peṟṟi vāyntatu. kaṟṟōrum maṟṟōrum iciyiṉ vayappaṭṭē niṟpar; aṉpip perukki āruyiri vaḷarppatu ici. iciyik kēṭṭu iṉpuṟāta uyirkaḷ illiyeṉṟē kūṟalām. vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, ceṭikaḷ, pāmpu mutaliya uyirkaḷ iciyil iṉpamaṭikiṉṟaṉa. pāl vēṇṭi aḻum pacuṅ kuḻaviyum ici vayappaṭṭup pāliyum paciyiyum maṟantu kaṇkaḷ ceruka makiḻcciyaṭikiṉṟatu. iciyiṉ vayappaṭātār aṉpiṉ vayappaṭār eṉṟē kūṟutal amiyum. ici vāḻkkiyil iṉpatti aḷikkum. iciyiṉ arumiyiyum perumiyiyum ōrntē tamiḻar icittamiḻi muttamiḻuḷ naṭunāyakamāka vittuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻ ilakkiya nūlkaḷ icit tamiḻilēyē irukkiṉṟaṉa.
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil ceyyappeṟṟa nūlkaḷil perumpālāṉavi ceyyuḷ vaṭivilēyē amintavi. piṟkālattil tōṉṟiya kalveṭṭukkaḷ yāvum aṅṅaṉamē amintuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṟkum piṟpaṭat tōṉṟiya añcal vaṭivil amintuḷḷa cīṭṭuk kavikaḷum mikkiruttalik kāṇa, ēṟattāḻap patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil tamiḻakattil icittamiḻ nūlkaḷē mikkiruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruntum.
tamiḻp piḷḷikaḷ tam kuḻantip paruvattil tālāṭṭup pākkaḷiyum ūcal pākkaḷiyum kēṭṭaṉar. viḷiyāṭum paruvattil pantu viḷiyāṭum pāṭalkaḷ (kantuka vari), ammāṉi, poṟ cuṇṇam, cāḻal, teḷḷēṇam, unti paṟattal, tōḷ nōkkam mutaliya āṭalkaḷukkuriya pāṭal kaḷip pāṭi makiḻntaṉar; maṇattil maṅkala vāḻttup pāṭalip pāṭiṉar; ulakki koṇṭu kuṟṟum toḻilukkuriya pāṭṭu vaḷḷippāṭṭu eṉṟa peyar peṟṟatu. ēṟṟam koṇṭu nīr iṟippavar pāṭiya ēṟṟappāṭṭu mutal uḻipput toḻilkaḷ aṉittiṟkum pāṭalkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu. tamiḻaṉ iṟanta piṉṉarum pāṭṭu avaṉi viṭavilli; palavakic cantaṅkaḷil oppāri pākkaḷ iṉṟum iruttalik kāṇalām. iṅṅaṉam paḻantamiḻar tam piṟappu mutal iṟappu variyil iciyilēyē vāḻntu vantaṉar eṉak kūṟutal muṟṟilum poruntum.
caṅka kālattil
tamiḻp paṇkaḷ ēḻu. avi kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiliruntu piṟappaṉa tiṟaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu piṟakkum paṇkaḷum tiṟaṅkaḷum pala kiḷikaḷāka iyaṅkum vakikaḷil eṇṇiṟanta ici vakikaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻp paṇkaḷ kuṟiñcip paṇ, pālip paṇ, mullip paṇ, marutap paṇ, neytaṟ paṇ eṉṟu perum paṇkaḷ aintu. ivaṟṟiṉ vakippaṭṭa paṇkaḷ pala. avaṟṟuḷ pakaṟ paṇkaḷ, iravup paṇkaḷ, kāli mālip paṇkaḷ eṉa avvap poḻutiṟkaminta paṇkaḷum vaḻakkiliruntaṉa.
paḻiya maṉitaṉ uṇarccip perukkāl pāṭiṉāṉ. koṇṭar, cavarar mutaliya anākarika makkaḷiṭam pāṭṭu mutaṉmi peṟṟiruppati iṉṟum kāṇalām. eṉavē, maṉita uḷḷattiṟkup pāṭṭu amutamākap payaṉpaṭṭatu; payaṉpaṭṭu varukiṟatu eṉpatu teḷivu. inta uṇmiyi uṇarntu kuṟiñci mutaliya nilaṅkaḷukku uriya karupporuḷkaḷik kūṟavanta tolkāppiyar mutaliya tol āciriyarkaḷ icik karuvi kaḷiyum paṇkaḷiyum cērttuk kūṟiṉar. tolkāppiyam- akattiṇiyiyalil ivi paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷik kāṇalām. kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ iṟi vaḻipāṭṭil iṉimiyākap pāṭikkoṇṭu kuṉṟak kuravi āṭiṉar; koṟṟaviyi vaḻipaṭap pāli nila makkaḷ piṟa karuvikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭutti vēṭṭuva vari pāṭiṉar. mulli nila āycciyar tām pāṭiya āycciyar kuraviyil mulli nilattiṟkuriya karuvikaḷiyum paṇkaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. maruta yāḻum, marutappaṇṇum maruta nilattiṟkuriyavi. neytal yāḻum, neytal paṇṇum neytal nilattiṟku uriyavi.
veṇpā, āciriyappā, kalippā, vañcippā eṉṉum nālvakip pākkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ iṉaṅkaḷum iciyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭavi. parintu varum iciyil pāṭuvatu paripāṭal. paṇ- irākam, pāṇ- pāṭṭu, pāṇar- pāṭakar, pāṭiṉiyar- peṇ pāṭakar, pāṇarum pāṭiṉiyarum icittamiḻp pulavarāvar. ivaruḷ cīṟiyāḻ, pēriyāḻ eṉṉum yāḻ vakikaḷi icittup pāṭuvōrum uḷar. avarkaḷ muṟiyē ciṟupāṇar eṉṟum perumpāṇar eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tam karuvikaḷi icittuk koṇṭē vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ pukaḻip pāṭip paricil peṟṟu vāḻntaṉar. paricil peṟṟa pāṇarkaḷ vaṟiya pāṭakarkaḷi vaḷḷalkaḷiṭam āṟṟuppaṭuttum aṟaneṟiyi mēṟ koṇṭiruntaṉar.
naṭaṉattiṟkum nāṭakattiṟkum ici iṉṟiyamiyātatu. ātalāl caṅka kālattil iciyāciriyar palar iruntaṉar. mātavi āṭiya naṭaṉa araṅkil iciyāciriyaṉ iruntāṉ eṉṟu cilappatikāram ceppukiṉṟatu. peṇkaḷ ulakki koṇṭu kuṟṟum poḻutum pantu āṭum  poḻutum ūcalāṭum poḻutum pāṭikkoṇṭē ceyalpaṭṭaṉar eṉpatic cilappatikāram vāḻttuk kātiyāl aṟiyalām. kaṭaṟkari ōrattiliruntu iṉpamākap pāṭum pāṭal varippāṭal eṉappaṭṭatu. atu kāṉal vari eṉavum peyar peṟṟatu. ap pāṭalkaḷic cilappatikārattil paṭittu makiḻalām.
	 maruṅku vaṇṭu ciṟantārppa
		maṇippū āṭi atupōrttuk
karuṅka yaṟkaṇ viḻittolki
		naṭantāy vāḻi kāvēri
karuṅka yaṟkaṇ viḻittolki
		naṭanta ellā niṉkaṇavaṉ
tiruntu ceṅkōl vaḷiyāmi
		aṟintēṉ vāḻi kāvēri
				(cilappatikāram, kāṉalvari)
pallavar kālattil
(ki.pi. 300-900)

ki.pi. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻakattil pallavarāṭci ēṟpaṭṭatu. vaṭa nāṭṭil kuptar āṭcik kālattil tōṉṟiya neṟi- āṭippāṭik kaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅkum pakti neṟi- tamiḻakattil paraviyatu. camaṇar, puttar, vitikar nuḻivālum celvākkālum vaṭamoḻi tamiḻakattil kāl koṇṭatu. vaṭa coṟkaḷum, vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum, vaḻipāṭṭu muṟiyum tamiḻil pukuntaṉa. inta māṟutalkaḷit tirunāvukkaracar mutaliya camaya kuravar pāṭalkaḷilum āḻvār pāṭalkaḷilum kāṇalām. kāntāram, takkēci, cātāri, kāntāra pañcuram, kucikam, pēka rākam mutaliya paṇ vakikaḷum catakam, tacāṅkam, patikam, yamakam mutaliyaṉavum vaṭavar kūṭṭuṟavāl nuḻintaviyākum.
tēvāra āciriyarum piṟa civap periyārkaḷum ici pāṭi iṟivaṉi makiḻvittaṉar; iṟivaṉiyē ici vaṭivattil kaṇṭaṉar. iyalavaṉ iciyavaṉ, paṇ avaṉ eṉṟellām nāyaṉmārkaḷ iṟivaṉip pārāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar. ñāṉacampantar tāḷamiṭṭup patikaṅkaḷip pāṭi iṟivaṉit talantoṟum ceṉṟu vaṇaṅkiṉār. tirunāvukkaracarum cuntaramum ippaṭiyē ici pāṭi iṟivaṉip pōṟṟiṉar. peṇkaḷum iṟivaṉuṭiya pala taṉmikaḷip pāṭikkoṇṭē kaḻal, pantu, ammāṉi mutaliya āṭṭaṅkaḷi āṭiṉar eṉṟu campantar pāṭal terivikkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟē iḷam peṇkaḷ pūk koytal, cuṇṇam iṭittal mutaliya pala vēlikaḷic ceytu koṇṭē iṟivaṉ ciṟappukkaḷi eṭuttuppāṭi makiḻtal paḻiya vaḻakkam eṉpatit tiruvācakap pāṭalkaḷ uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.
māṭukaḷi mēyttu vanta āṉāya nāyaṉār pullāṅ kuḻalil ainteḻuttiṉi ōti iṟivaṉi aṭintār eṉṟu periya purāṇam pēcukiṉṟatu. tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇarum, tiruppāṇāḻvārum yāḻ mīṭṭiyum pāṭiyum pēṟu peṟṟaṉar eṉṟu nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
camaya kuravar pākkaḷ pala kolli, intaḷam, cīkāmaram, kuṟiñci, naṭṭapāṭi, viyāḻak kuṟiñci, cevvaḻi, puṟanīrmi mutaliya  tamiḻp paṇkaḷil pāṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tirunāvukkaracar īcaṉ entaṉ iṇiyaṭi nīḻal mācillāta vīṇiyoli pōṉṟatu, eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. itiliruntu avaruṭiya icip pulamiyiyum ici iṉpattil āḻntu kiṭanta nuṭpa uṇarviṉiyum nām naṉku aṟiyalām. civa viṇava camayaṅkaḷ pallavar kālattil mikutiyākap paravuvataṟkuriya ciṟanta kāraṇaṅkaḷuḷ icip pākkaḷum oṉṟākum. pallavap peru nāṭṭilirunta peruṅ kōvilkaḷil ici veḷḷam kari puraṇṭu ōṭiyatu eṉpatit tirumuṟip pāṭalkaḷ koṇṭu uṇaralām.
	paṇṇiyal pāṭal aṟāta āvūr
	pattimip pāṭal aṟāta āvūr
	pāiyal pāṭal aṟāta āvūr
	tiyalār pāṭṭu ōvāc cāykkāṭu
	mātar mintar icipāṭum pūmpukār
ivaṟṟāl peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum iciyil ciṟantiruntaṉar eṉṉum uṇmiyi aṟiyalām.
tēvāram kuṟikkum icik karuvikaḷ
yāḻ, kuḻal, kiṉṉari, kokkari, caccari, takki, muḻavam, monti, mirutaṅkam, mattaḷam, tamarukam, tuntupi, kuṭamuḻā, tattalakam, muracam, uṭukki, tāḷam, tuṭi, koṭukoṭṭi mutaliyaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ pala caṅka kālattilēyē tamiḻakattil iruntavi. vīṇi, tamarukam, mirutaṅkam, tuntupi eṉpaṉa vaṭavar cērkkiyāl nuḻintavi eṉṟu koḷḷalām.
āḻvār aruṭpāṭalkaḷum ivvāṟē tamiḻppaṇkaḷilum vaṭamoḻip paṇkaḷilum pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
makēntiravarmaṉ, irāca cimmaṉ mutaliya pallava maṉṉarkaḷ iciyil perum pulami peṟṟiruntaṉar. kuṭumiyāṉ maliyiluḷḷa ici paṟṟiya kalveṭṭu appar kālattavaṉāṉa makēntiraṉ veṭṭuvittatākum. ak kalveṭṭuc ceytikaḷ avaṉatu iciyaṟivi naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. caṅkakālattil illāta vīṇi pallavar varavāl tamiḻakattil nuḻintatu. ivi eṭṭiṟkum ēḻiṟkum uriya eṉṟu kuṭumiyāṉ malik kalveṭṭu ici paṟṟiya ceytikaḷik kūṟuvatāl ēḻu narampukaḷiyuṭiya vīṇi, eṭṭu narampukaḷiyuṭiya vīṇi eṉpaṉa akkālattil iruntaṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ēḻu narampukaḷiyuṭiya vīṇiyē eṅkum iruppatu. eṭṭu narampukaḷiyuṭiya vīṇiyi makēntiraṉ putitākak kaṇṭu piṭittāṉ pōlum! makēntira varmaṉ parivātiṉi eṉṉum peyar koṇṭa vīṇiyi vācippatil vallavaṉāka iruntāṉ. oru peṇ taṉ tōḻiyi aṇittuk koṇṭu paṭuppatu pōla maṅki orutti parivātiṉiyi aṇittuk koṇṭu uṟaṅkiṉāḷ. anta vīṇi poṉ narampukaḷi yuṭiyatu eṉṟu acuvakōṣar putta caritattil kūṟiyuḷḷār. ik kūṟṟik koṇṭu, makēntiraṉ payaṉpaṭuttiya putiya vīṇi ettakiyatu eṉpati oruvāṟu aṟiyalām.
irāca cimmaṉ vātya vityātaraṉ (icik karuvi icippatil vityātaraṉi ottavaṉ), atōtya tumpura (vīṇi, muracam, kuḻal, tāḷam ivaṟṟil tumpuruvi ottavaṉ), vīṇā nārataṉ (vīṇi vācippatil nārataṉip pōṉṟavaṉ) eṉṟu kāñci kayilācanātar kōvil kalveṭṭukkaḷ kuṟittuḷḷaṉa.
cōḻar kālattil
(ki.pi. 900-1300)
vaṭavar kūṭṭuṟavāl palavakic cantaṅkaḷ tamiḻil pukuntaṉa. vaṭukac cantam, karunāṭakac cantam mutaliya cantaṅkaḷ yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuriyil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vētac culōkaṅkaḷ oruvakic cantattil pāṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟip pāṭiyōr āriyam pāṭuvār eṉap paṭṭaṉar.
cintāmaṇi
oṉpatām tirumuṟiyiluḷḷa pākkaḷ tiru icip pākkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟākiya cīvaka cintāmaṇiyiṉ talivaṉ cīvakaṉ.  avaṉ, āṇkaḷip pārkkalākātu eṉṟu irunta curamañcari eṉṟa peṇṇik kiḻa vēṭattuṭaṉ ceṉṟu icipāṭi veṉṟāṉ, avaṉ pāṭiya iciyik kēṭṭatum peṇkaḷ, vēṭaṉ paṟavi pōlak kattum ōciyik kēṭṭu mayaṅkik kūṭṭamāka ōṭivarum mayilkaḷip pōla ōṭi vantaṉarām
periya purāṇam
periya purāṇattiluḷḷa āṉāya nāyaṉār purāṇattil pullāṅkuḻal ceyyappaṭum muṟiyiyum pullāṅkuḻal vācikkum muṟiyiyum viḷakkamākak kāṇalām. āṉāyar pullāṅkuḻalil ainteḻutti vācitta muṟiyic cēkkiḻār mikavum viḷakkamāka ēḻu ceyyuṭkaḷil (22-28) pāṭiyuḷḷār. pul aruntiya pacuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷ ak kuḻalōciyik kēṭṭu āṉāyari aṭintu tammi maṟantu niṉṟaṉa. pāl parukik koṇṭirunta pacukkaṉṟukaḷ pāl uṇṇum toḻili maṟantaṉa. erutukaḷum māṉ mutaliya kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷum mayir mukiḻttu vantu avar pakkattil aṇintaṉa; āṭik koṇṭirunta mayilkaḷ aciviṉṟi avariṭam vantaṉa; piṟa paṟavikaḷum ici vayappaṭṭut tammi maṟantu viṭṭaṉa. piṟa kōvalarkaḷum ici kēṭṭa aḷavil tam toḻili viṭuttu niṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu āciriyar iciyiṉ ciṟappi eṭuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār (ceyyuḷ 30-31).
tirunāvukkaracaratu iṟutik kālattil āṭalilum pāṭalilum valla aḻakiya maṅkiyaric civaperumāṉ avariṭam aṉuppi, avaratu aḻuntiya paktiyic cōtikka virumpiṉār. am maṅkiyar tirunāvukkaracar muṉṉiliyil tōṉṟi, uriya kāṉaṅkaḷiliruntu niṟintu varum iciyāl uṇṭākum takka iṉimiyuṭaṉ ici amutip parukac ceyyum kovvik kaṉivāyit tiṟantu nīla malar pōṉṟa neṭiya kaṇkaḷi veḷiyil parappi iciyiṉip pāṭalāyiṉar.
kampa rāmāyaṇam
ittakiya perumi vāynta iciyippaṟṟik kampar perumāṉ yātu kūṟiyuḷḷār eṉpatik kavaṉippōm; nāṭṭup paṭalattil maruta nilattilum neytal nilattilum eḻukiṉṟa icikaḷ oṉṟupaṭuvati aṅkuḷḷa uyirkaḷē aṉṟik kuṟiñci mulli nilaṅkaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷum maṟṟiya uyirkaḷum anupavittu uṟaṅkum tiṟaṉik kūṟum ceyyuḷ iṉpam payakkiṉṟatu.
	 koṉṟi vēyṅkuḻal kōvalar muṉṟilil
	kaṉṟu(u)ṟaṅkum kuravi kaṭiciyar
	puṉṟa lippuṉam kāppuṭip pōtarac
	ceṉṟi cikkum nuḻicciyar cevvaḻi.
maruta nilattil uḻattiyar pāṭum kuravip pāṭṭu mullinila iṭiyar icikkum kuḻaliṉ iciyōṭu taḻuvi avar muṉṟilil kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum kaṉṟukaḷit tūṅkac ceyyum; neytal nilattu nuḻicciyar pāṭukiṉṟa cevvaḻi eṉṟa paṇṇil pāṭum pāṭṭit tiṉik kolliyil kāval kākkum kuṟiñci nilappeṇkēṭṭu iṉiya tuyil koḷvāḷ. ataṉāl tiṉippuṉak kāval aḻikiṉṟatu; katirkaḷik kiḷikaḷ koḷḷi koḷkiṉṟaṉa.
pāṭakar iṉiya matuvi uṇṭu tamatu ciṟiya yāḻi icittut teḷviḷi eṉṉum paṇṇip pāṭik koṇṭu vikaṟiyil teru vaḻiyē celvārkaḷ. ap pāṭalē kōcala nāṭṭu makaḷirit tuyiliṉiṉṟu eḻuppuvatākum.
	 teḷḷic cīṟiyāḻp pāṇar tēṉpiḻi naṟavam mānti
	vaḷvici karuvi pampa vayiṉvayiṉ vaḻaṅku pāṭal
	veḷḷiveṇ māṭat tumpar veyilviri pacumpoṉ paḷḷi
	eḷḷarum karuṅkaṇ tōki iṉtuyil eḻuppum aṉṟē
mitilik kāṭcip paṭalattik kūṟappōnta kampar iciyip perumippaṭuttaṟku iṭam kiṭattamikku makiḻntu tam eṇṇattil iruntatit teḷḷitil terivittu viṭṭār. ici payil cālikaḷum nāṭaka mēṭikaḷum mitiliyil uṇṭu; narampuk karuvikaḷi mīṭṭum vakiyum, pāṭum vakiyum pāṭuvār kavaṉikka vēṇṭuvatiyum kampar kīḻ varumāṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār:
	 vaḷḷukirt taḷirkki nōva māṭakam paṟṟi vārnta
kaḷḷeṉa narampu vīkkik kaṇṇoṭu maṉamum kūṭṭit
teḷḷiya muṟuval tōṉṟa virunteṉa makaḷir ītta
teḷviḷip pāṇit tīntēṉ cevimaṭut tiṉitu ceṉṟār.
yāḻiṉ narampukaḷit tam kūriya nakaṅkaḷ vāynta taḷir pōṉṟa kikaḷ nōkumāṟu valittuc curuti cērttu, tām pāṭum pāṭaliṉ poruḷi maṉattil anupavittu, avvāṟu anupavippatu avar kaṇkaḷil tōṉṟavum, iḷanaki uṇṭāyatāl palloḷi ciṟitu viḷaṅkavum, teḷviḷi eṉṉum iciyil aminta pāṭṭākiya tēṉi irāma lakkumaṇarukkum kōcikaṉukkum viruntākat tantaṉar. avarkaḷ ceviyāra uṇṭu iṉitu pōṉār. curuti cērttal- pāṭum pāṭṭi uḷḷavāṟu uṇartal- uṇarntati urukkamāy anupavittal- anupavittatāl uṇṭākum iṉpattik kaṇkaḷil tōṟṟuvittal- ici iṉpam tōṉṟa iḷanaki arumputal ākiya ivi yaṉittum ici pāṭuvōriṭam tōṉṟa vēṇṭuvaṉa eṉpatik kampar namakku aṟivuṟuttu kiṉṟār.
kalveṭṭuc ceytikaḷ
cōḻar kālak kalveṭṭukkaḷ pallāyirakkaṇak kāṉavi. avi ici paṟṟiya ceytikaḷi namakku uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. periya kōvilkaḷil pāṭal makaḷirum iruntaṉar. nāyaṉmār pākkaḷiyum āḻvārkaḷiṉ aruṭ pāṭalkaḷiyum kōvilkaḷil pāṭa ōtuvārkaḷum pāṭal makaḷirum iruntaṉar. yāḻ vācittu ip pāṭalkaḷip pāṭum pāṇarkaḷum cōḻar kālattil iruntaṉar. cōḻar kālattil nāṭakak kali, kaṭṭaṭak kali, ōviyak kali, ciṟpak kali, naṭaṉak kali eṉpavi uyarnta muṟiyil vaḷarcci peṟṟaṉa. irāca rāca nāṭakam, irāca rācēcuvara nāṭakam, pūmpuliyūr nāṭakam mutaliya nāṭakaṅkaḷ naṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru periya kōvililum camayac cārpāṉa pāṭalkaḷuk kēṟpa naṭaṉa maṅkiyar iruntu naṭittaṉar eṉṟu kalveṭṭukkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. naṭaṉattiṟkum nāṭakattiṟkum ici uyir nāṭiyākum. eṉavē, icikkali cōḻar kālattil mikavum uyarntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu eḷitil tuṇiyappaṭum.
piṟkālattil
vicaya nakara āṭciyil ki.pi. 14- ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil alāvutīṉ kiljiyiṉ paṭittalivarāṉa mālik- kāpūr teṉṉāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttār. vinta malikkut teṉpāṟpaṭṭa yātava aracu, kākatīya aracu, hoycala aracu, pāṇṭiya aracu eṉpavi ac cēṉit talivar muṉ tali tāḻntaṉa. tamiḻakam avaratu paṭi yeṭuppāl nili taḷarntatu. muculimkaḷit taṭuttu niṟutti intukkaḷik kākka vēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇarcciyuṭaṉ pallāri māvaṭṭattil vicaya nakara aracu ēṟpaṭṭatu. anta aracarkaḷ kaṉṉaṭattiyum teluṅkiyum vaḷarttaṉar; āntira iciyi yum karunāṭaka iciyiyum vaḷarttaṉar. nāḷaṭivil inta iraṇṭum oṉṟākik karunāṭaka ici eṉṟē vaḷaralāyiṟṟu.
tuṅkapattiri āṟu mutal kaṉṉiyākumari vari uḷḷa nāṭu vicayanakara vēntar āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭatu. vicaya nakara vēntarkku aṭaṅkiya nāyakkarāṭci maturiyilum tañcāvūrilum ceñciyilum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl āntirarum karunāṭakarum tamiḻ nāṭṭu aracāṅka aluvalarākat teṟkē kuṭiyēṟiṉar. inta māṟutalkaḷāl tamiḻakattil karunāṭaka ici nuḻintu paravat toṭaṅkiyatu. iṅṅaṉam ēṟpaṭṭa āṭci muṟi māṟupāṭṭāl putiya maṉṉarkaḷi iṉpuṟuttip paricu peṟat tamiḻici vāṇar karunāṭaka iciyik kaṟkalāyiṉar. ip putiya pōkkiṉāl, tamiḻakattil vāḻiyaṭi vāḻiyāka iruntu vanta tamiḻici ciṟitu ciṟitāka maṟintatu. nāṭṭil teluṅkup pāṭalkaḷ celvākkup peṟṟaṉa.
piṟanāṭṭu icikaḷ
iculām, kiṟittavam eṉṉum iraṇṭu putiya camayaṅkaḷiṉ nuḻivāl ac camayaṅkaḷit taḻuviya tamiḻar intutāṉi iciyum mēṉāṭṭu iciyum kaṟkalāyiṉar. kiṟittavar kōvilkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum mēṉāṭṭu iciyē iruntu varukiṟatu. anta iciyil tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷum pāṭappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟē muculimkaḷ intutāṉi iciyi virumpik kaṟkiṉṟaṉar.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil
āṅkila āṭciyil pēṉṭu (Band) ici tamiḻakattil paraviyatu. iṉṟum pala tirumaṇaṅkaḷil avviciyi nām kēṭkiṟōm. nam nāṭṭu nātavaram aḻiyātu iruntu varukiṟatu. paṇṭip kālap paṟi, caṅku, uṭukki, tāḷam, kompu mutaliya icik karuvikaḷ ciṟṟūrkaḷil nilittu iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. ēṟṟam iṟikkum poḻutum, nāṟṟu naṭumpoḻutum, kaṉamāṉa poruḷi iḻuttuc cellum poḻutum, neṭuntūram vaṇṭikaḷi ōṭṭic cellum poḻutum, cuṇṇāmpu iṭikkum poḻutum avvattoḻi lil īṭupaṭṭa āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum pākkaḷip pāṭukiṟārkaḷ. ivvāṟu pāṭum paḻakkam caṅkakālam mutalē iruntu varum paḻakkamākum eṉpati muṉṉarē kaṇṭōm. kuḻantiyi uṟaṅka vikkum tālāṭṭup pāṭalum paḻami yāṉatu.
ēṟattāḻap pattāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ variyil tamiḻakattu iṉṉici araṅkukaḷil teluṅkup pāṭalkaḷē āṭci purintu vantaṉa. tamiḻ makkaḷ naṭuvil tamiḻ icivāṇar teluṅkup pāṭalkaḷiyē pāṭi vantaṉar. ovvōr iciyaraṅkiṉ iṟutiyil maṭṭum iraṇṭoru tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷip pāṭivantaṉar. tamiḻp paṟṟuṭiya nam makkaḷ iv veṭkak kēṭāṉa nilimiyik kaṇṭu maṉam varuntiṉar; ceyvaki tōṉṟātu viḻittaṉar.
anta niliyil ceṭṭi nāṭṭaracar irācā car. aṇṇāmalic ceṭṭiyār tamiḻiciyi vaḷarkka muṟpaṭṭār. avarukku utaviyāka ṭākṭar ār.kē. caṇmukam ceṭṭiyārum, car.em. ē. muttiyā ceṭṭiyārum nallaṟiñar palarum iruntaṉar. ivar tam muyaṟciyāl tamiḻakattup pērūrkaḷil tamiḻicic caṅkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. talimic caṅkam ceṉṉiyil niṟuvappaṭṭatu. palar toṭakkattil tamiḻici iyakkatti etirttaṉar. aracarum piṟarum maṉam taḷarātu tamiḻiciyi vaḷarkkat toṭaṅkavē, mutalil tōṉṟiya etirppu maṟintatu. aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattil tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷ pala nūlkaḷ vaṭivil veḷivantaṉa- iṉṟum vantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvōr āṇṭum ṭicampar iṟutiyil ceṉṉiyil tamiḻici mānāṭu naṭipeṟṟu varukiṟatu. tamiḻici vāṇar kuḻuvil tamiḻp paṇkaḷ ārāyappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. pērāciriyar cāmpamūrtti, cittūr cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷi, taṇṭapāṇi tēcikar mutaliya icivāṇarkaḷ ivvārāycciyil ūkkam kāṭṭi varukiṉ ṟaṉar. caṅka nūlkaḷi naṉku ārāyntu paḻiya yāḻkaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷi vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ yāḻ nūl eṉṉum peyaril eḻutiyuḷḷār. tamiḻici potu makkaḷiṉ ātaravip peṟṟuviṭṭatāl, teluṅkup pāṭalkaḷiyē pāṭikkoṇṭirunta icivāṇarum ippoḻutu tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷip pāṭi varukiṉṟaṉar.
nāṭakamum paṭakkāṭciyum
tamiḻ makkaḷ nāṭakak kaliyiṉ uyarvi ippoḻutu uṇarntuvarukiṉṟaṉar. camayam, varalāṟu, cīrtiruttam ivaṟṟit taḻuviya nāṭakaṅkaḷ iṉṟu naṭikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ṭi.kē. e. cakōtararkaḷ, civāji kaṇēcaṉ, em.ji. irāmac cantiraṉ, kē.ār. irāmacāmi , e. e. irājēntiraṉ, taṅkavēlu, tēvi nāṭaka capiyār, navāp irācamāṇikkam mutaliya nāṭakak kaliñarkaḷit toṭarntu palarum palavaki nāṭakaṅ kaḷi naṭittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟilellām tamiḻici muḻakkamiṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻp paṭakkāṭcikaḷ mikac ciṟanta muṟiyil icik kaliyi vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉa. kālattiṟkēṟṟa karuttaminta pāṭṭukaḷ paṭakkāṭciyuḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷami kēṭṭu makiḻtaṟkuriyatu.
kaṭcip pāṭalkaḷ
iṉṟiya tamiḻakattiluḷḷa araciyal kaṭcikaḷum camutāya nalak kaṭcikaḷum tattam koḷkikaḷukkēṟṟa pāṭalkaḷi iyaṟṟik kaṭcik kūṭṭaṅkaḷil pāṭukiṉṟaṉa; ap pāṭalkaḷ vāyilākat tam koḷkikaḷip potu makka ḷiṭiyē parappukiṉṟaṉa. pāratiyār pākkaḷit terukkaḷil pāṭiyatu, kāṅkira kaṭci mikka celvākkup peṟṟamikku oru kāraṇamākum.
vāṉoliyil tamiḻici
potuvākac ceṉṉi vāṉoli niliyattilum, ciṟappākat tirucci vāṉoli niliyattilum tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷ nāḷtōṟum pāṭappeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻici yilum karunāṭaka iciyilum mēṉāṭṭu iciyilum arapu nāṭṭu iciyilum tamiḻppākkaḷ uruvākik koṇṭē varukiṉṟaṉa. inta vaḷarcci varavēṟkattakkatu.
muṭivuri
oru nāṭṭil vēṟṟaracar āṭciyāl pala tuṟikaḷilum māṟutal ēṟpaṭutal iyaṟki. tamiḻakattilum piṟa tuṟikaḷ pōlavē icit tuṟiyilum im māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. mutalil iruntatu tamiḻici; piṟaku vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ici tamiḻil kalantatu. piṉpu vicayanakara āṭciyiṉ viḷivāl karunāṭaka ici kāl koṇṭatu. muculimkaḷ āṭciyāl intutāṉi ici nuḻintatu. piṉṉar āṅkilēyar āṭciyāl mēṉāṭṭu ici muṟi tamiḻakattil paraviyatu. āṭci māṟupaṭṭāl camutāyat tuṟi palavaṟṟilum māṟupāṭukaḷ ēṟpaṭuvatu pōlavē kalit tuṟiyilum māṟupāṭukaḷ ēṟpaṭutal iyaṟkiyē aṉṟō!

7. naṭaṉak kali
kūttiṉ tōṟṟam
iṟivaṉatu aruṭ paṭippilē kūttu nikaḻkiṉṟatu. evariṭamum payiṟci peṟāmalum, kūttuk kaliyiṉ paṇpic ciṟitaḷavum kallāmalum, karu mukilkaḷik kāṇum mayilkaḷ tam ciṟakukaḷi virittu āṭukiṉṟaṉa. koṭiya aravaṅkaḷ makuṭi ōciyik kēṭṭatum tam puṟṟukkaḷiliruntu veḷippōntu iciyil īṭupaṭṭu meymaṟantu paṭameṭuttu āṭukiṉṟaṉa. kaṇṇaṉip pōṉṟāratu vēyṅ kuḻalil īṭupaṭṭuk kaṉṟu kālikaḷ tammi maṟantavaṉavāy acintāṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟik kūrntu nōkkiṉ, iyaṟkip paṭippilēyē kūttuiyalpāka amintuḷḷa uṇmiyi uṇaralām aṉṟō?
mali muḻikaḷil vāḻnta paṇṭi makkaḷ mutaṟkaṇ palavāṟāka ōcikaḷiyiṭṭu oruvarukkoruvar tam eṇṇaṅkaḷi aṟivittukkoṇṭaṉar. piṉṉar am makkaḷ cikikaḷ cērttut tam karuttukkaḷi veḷippaṭuttiṉar; piṉṉarp paṭippaṭiyākat taṅkaṭkeṉa moḻiyi amittuk koṇṭaṉar. ivvāṟu ovvoru nāṭṭup paṇṭi makkaḷum taṅkaṭkeṉat taṉittaṉi moḻiyi amittukkoṇṭaṉar. maṉitaṉ palavaki uṇarccikaḷ koṇṭavaṉ; aḷavu kaṭanta makiḻcci uṇṭākum poḻutu avaṉ taṉṉi maṟantu kūttāṭuvāṉ; kōpamāyirukkum poḻutu kaṇ civantu utaṭukaḷ tuṭikkap paraparappuṭaṉ pēcuvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam maṉitaṉ ovvōr  niliyil oṉṟi veḷippaṭiyākak kāṭṭutal allatu ceytal iyalpu.
maṉitaṉ makiḻcci niliyil taṉ viruppappaṭi palavaki ōcikaḷi eḻuppa- avvōcikaḷ, cila camayaṅkaḷil avaṉiyaṟiyātu avaṉukku iṉpatti aḷikka- avaṉ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum avvōcikaḷi eḻuppi anupavikka- ivvāṟu eḻuppappaṭṭa olikaḷiṉ cērkki kātukku iṉpamaḷitta niliyil, avaṉip piṉpaṟṟip piṟar at tuṟiyil iṟaṅkiyirukkalām.
maṉitaṉ taṉ kūttukkēṟpa olikaḷi eḻuppiyum, kikaḷik koṭṭiyumirukkalām. avaṉ iṅṅaṉam kutittatu kūttākavum, olikaḷi eḻuppi ōciyiṭṭatu iciyākavum, kikaḷik koṭṭiyatu tāḷamākavum māṟiyirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.
ivvāṟu piṟantirukkalām eṉṟu karutukiṉṟa ici, kūttu, tāḷam ākiya mūṉṟum cērnta tokuppē kūttu allatu naṭaṉam eṉpatu.
intiyāvukku veḷiyē
mikap paḻiya ekiptiya- cumēriya- pāpilōṉiya nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷil pala vaki naṭaṉaṅkaḷi uṇarttum ōviyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl ap paṇṭi nākarikaṅkaḷil tiḷitta makkaḷ kūttuk kaliyil paṇpaṭṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu.
intiyāvil
intiyāvil vētakālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa cintuveḷi nākarikatti uṇarttum puti poruḷkaḷil veṇkalattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa naṭaṉamātiṉ vaṭivam oṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷatu. cila kaṟcilikaḷ naṭaṉa muṟiyil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tavulik kaḻuttil māṭṭit toṅkaviṭṭa maṉita uruvam (kaḷimaṇṇiṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu) kiṭittuḷḷatu. mirutaṅkam, vīṇi, tāḷam ivaṟṟik kuṟikkum ōviyaṅkaḷ akappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟic cērttu nōkkumpoḻutu, cintuveḷi makkaḷ naṭaṉak kaliyil aṟivuṭiyavarāka iruntaṉar eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟa taṉṟō!
vaṭa intiyāvil naṭaṉattip paṟṟi eḻunta mutal vaṭamoḻi nūl paratam eṉpatu. itaṉic ceytavar parata muṉivar. atu ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatu eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. iḵtu iṉṟaḷavum mikac ciṟanta nūlāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu. ittakiya ciṟanta nūl ki.mu. 3-ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉatu eṉiṉ, ataṟku muṉṉarē vaṭa intiyāvil naṭaṉakkali mika uyarnta niliyi aṭintiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṟataṉṟō? naṭaṉak kali, aṟam- poruḷ- iṉpam- vīṭu eṉṉum nāṟ pēṟukaḷiyum nalka vallatu; maṉavuṟuti, uṭalvaḷam, iṉpa uṇarcci mutaliya naṟpaṇpukaḷi aḷikka vallatu. ik kali tarum iṉpam tavattiṉar aṭiyum iṉpattiṟku iṇiyāṉatu eṉpatu paratattiṟ kūṟappaṭum ceytiyākum.
paratam tōṉṟiya piṉṉar eḻunta vaṭanāṭṭu ōviyaṅ kaḷilum, ciṟpaṅkaḷilum, ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum naṭaṉakkali naṉku kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kuptarkaḷ āṭcik kālattil eḻunta ayalār paṭiyeṭuppukkaḷālum kuḻappaṅkaḷālum vaṭa intiyāvil ik kali naṉku vaḷara iṭamaṟṟatu. āyiṉ, vintamalikku teṉpāṟpaṭṭa nāṭukaḷil ikkali naṉṉiliyil vaḷarcci peṟalāyiṟṟu.
caṅka kālattil kūttukkali
iṉṟuḷḷa caṅka nūlkaḷil tolkāppiyam paḻiya teṉpatip perumpālōr oppukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉ kuṟinta kālam ēṟattāḻak ki.mu. 300 eṉṉal tavaṟākātu. ip paḻiya nūlil paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṭam iruntuvanta kūttu vakikaḷ cila kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi (1) vēlaṉ āṭum (kāntaṉ) veṟikkūttu, (2) veṉṟavīrar tam aṭiyāḷap pūccūṭṭi āṭum karuṅkūttu, (3) veṟṟiyip pārāṭṭip peṇkaḷ āṭum vaḷḷikkūttu, (4) kaḷatti viṭṭu ōṭāta iḷiya vīraṉukkuk kālil kaḻalikkaṭṭi irupālarum āṭum kaḻaṉilikkūttu mutaliyaṉa ākum. ivi perumpālum pōrttoṭar pāṉavi. iviyaṉṟi āycciyar kuravi, vēṭṭuva vari mutaliya vakikaḷum iruntaṉa.
kuṟiñci, mulli, neytal, marutam mutaliya nāṉilaṅ kaḷilum avvan nilattiṟkuriya kūttar iruntu vantaṉar, kūttukkaḷ naṭipeṟṟu vantaṉa eṉpatic caṅka nūlkaḷāl nām aṟiyalām. kuṟiñci nilattil kuṟappeṇkaḷ taṅkaḷ kuṟiñci nilak kiḻavaṉākiya murukap perumāṉi vaḻipaṭṭu āṭum kūttu, kuṉṟak kuravi allatu kuravi eṉappaṭum ituvē mullinila makaḷirāl māyōṉit tutittu āṭappaṭumāyiṉ, āycciyar kuravi eṉap peyar peṟum. kuravi eṉpatu eḻuvar maṅkiyar cennili maṇṭalak kaṭakak kikōttu an nilikkoppa niṉṟu āṭum kūttākum. kuraviyāṭutal iru pālarukkum uriyatu. kuṟavar toṇṭakap paṟi koṭṭak kuraviyāṭuvar. atu kāṇum makaḷirum mēṟcoṉṉa ilakkaṇappaṭi niṉṟu āṭuvar. aṅṅaṉam āṭumpōtu avarkaḷ pāṭikkoṇṭē āṭuvar. itaṉ vivaraṅkaḷit toki nūlkaḷilum cilappatikārattilum virivākak kāṇalām.
tuṇaṅki eṉpatu kūttu vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. atu muṭaṅkiya iru kikaḷiyum vilāppuṭikaḷil oṟṟi aṭittuk koṇṭu acintāṭum oruvakik kūttu. makaḷir viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil tuṇaṅkik kūttāṭuvar. āṭavar am makaḷirkku mutaṟki koṭuttal vaḻakkam.
nāṉilaṅkaḷilum irunta vaḷḷalkaḷpāṟ ceṉṟu, uḷḷak kuṟippup puṟattu veḷippaṭa āṭiya naṭaṉamaṅki paṇṭit tamiḻakattil viṟali eṉap peṟṟāḷ. iv viṟaliyar pāṇaruṭaṉum kūttaruṭaṉum ceṉṟu vaḷḷalkaḷiṭam paricu peṟṟu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ perumpālum pāṇaruṭaṉ celvatu vaḻakkam. viṟali āṭu makaḷ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟāḷ. (kuṟuntoki, 105)
iṅṅaṉam meyppāṭukaḷ tōṉṟa naṭikkum viṟaliyar caṅka kālattil palar iruntaṉar eṉpatit tokinūṟkaḷāl aṟiyalām. paṇṭi aracarkaḷ viṟaliyar āṭar pāṭalkaḷip pārāṭṭip poṉṉari māliyip paricaḷittaṉar; toṭi mutaliya ciṟanta nakikaḷiyum vaḻaṅkiṉar (puṟam-364, 105).  viṟaliyar  niṟamuṭiya kalavi pūcappaṭṭa vaḷinta cantiṉi yuṭiya muṉkikaḷiyum oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyiyum uṭiyavar eṉṟu kōvūrkiḻār pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār. viṟaliyar, yāḻ vācittāṟ pōlap pāṭum nayappāṭu tōṉṟum pāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya viṟalpaṭa āṭutaliyuṭiyār eṉṟu māṅkuṭi marutaṉārum peruṅkavucikaṉārum kūṟal, viṟaliya ruṭiya icic ciṟappiṉiyum naṭaṉap payiṟciyiṉ mēṉmiyiṉiyum naṉku uṇarttuvatākum. (maturik kāñci, vari 210-18, uri; malipaṭukaṭām, vari. 534-6, nac.uri) cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi (ittakiya) viṟaliyarkku māṭa maturiyiyō vañcimā nakarattiyō vēṇṭumāyiṉ taravallavaṉ eṉṟu kōvūrkiḻār kūṟiyuḷḷatiliruntu,  viṟaliyar paṇṭi aracarpāl peṟṟirunta peruñ ciṟappiṉik kāṇalām. (puṟam-32)  viṟali kūtti, āṭumakaḷ, āṭukaḷa makaḷ eṉavum nūlkaḷiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭāḷ. aṅṅaṉamē naṭaṉamāṭupavaṉ kūttaṉ, āṭumakaṉ, āṭukaḷamakaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. ivarkaḷ caṅka kālattiṟ kūttukkaliyi vaḷartta kalivāṇar āvar. ivarkaḷ naṭaṉamāṭiya araṅkam āṭukaḷam eṉappaṭṭatu. viḻākkālaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ palavakik kūttukkaḷi āṭiṉārkaḷ; piṟa kālaṅkaḷil aracar mutaliya vaḷḷiyōṟ pāṟ ceṉṟu, pāṭi āṭip paricil peṟṟu mīṇṭārkaḷ. ivarkaḷatu kaliyi uṇarttum nūlē kūtta nūl eṉpatu.
cilappatikārattil
cilappatikārattil kūttu eṉpatu nāṭakam eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vaḻaṅkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. nāṭaka makaḷir eṉṉum toṭar naṭaṉa makaḷir, kūtta makaḷir, viṟaliyar eṉṉum peyarkaḷiṉ poruḷiyē aḷippatākum. mātavi nāṭaka maṅki (viṟali); avaḷ āṭiya kaḷam nāṭaka araṅku eṉappeyar peṟṟatu. ip peyarkaḷ vaṭavar nuḻiviṉāl uṇṭāṉa peyar māṟṟaṅkaḷ eṉṉalām. cilappatikārattil naṭaṉak kaliyip paṟṟiya pala vivaraṅkaḷ araṅkēṟṟu kātiyil viḷakkamākat tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
naṭaṉa makaḷ ciṟappākak kūttu, pāṭṭu, aḻaku im mūṉṟilum niṟivu peṟṟavaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum. avaḷ 7 vayatu mutal 12 vayatu vari (ēṟattāḻa āṟāṇṭukaḷ) kūttuk kaliyiṟ payiṟci peṟutal vēṇṭum. naṭaṉa makkaḷip payiṟṟuvikkum āṭal āciriyaṉ iruvakik kūttukkaḷil vallavaṉāka iruttal vēṇṭum iruvakik kūttāvaṉa: vacik kūttu- pukaḻk kūttu; vēttiyal- potuviyal; varikkūttu- variccāntik kūttu; cāntik kūttu- vinōtak kūttu; āriyam- tamiḻ; iyalpuk kūttu- tēcikak kūttu eṉap palavaki. ivi virinta nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇka eṉavarum aṭiyārkku nallār uri kāṇattakkatu. cāntikkūttu eṉpatu talivaṉ iṉpamēnti niṉṟu āṭiya kūttu. atu (1) cokkam, (2)mey, (3)apinayam, (4) nāṭakam eṉa nālvakip paṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ (1) cokkam eṉpatu tūya naṭaṉam. atu 108 karaṇamuṭiyatu; (2) meykkūttu eṉpatu tēci, vaṭuku, ciṅkaḷam eṉa mūvakippaṭum. ivi meyttoḻiṟ kūttākaliṉ meykkūttāyiṉa. ivi akaccuvi paṟṟi eḻutaliṉakamārkkam eṉappaṭum; (3) apinayakkūttu eṉpatu, kati taḻuvātu pāṭṭiṉatu poruḷukkuk kikkāṭṭi vallapam ceyyum palavakik kūttu; (4) nāṭakam eṉpatu kati taḻuvi varum kūttu... eṉavarum aṭiyārkku nallār uriyum avar mēlum araṅkēṟṟu kātiyil tarum pala viparaṅkaḷum ūṉṟip paṭittu uṇaraṟ pālaṉa. avi cilappatikāra kālattil irunta kūttuk kaliyiṉ vaḷarcciyit teḷivāka uṇarttum ceytikaḷākum.
vīram, accam, iḻivu, viyappu, iṉpam, tuṉpam, naki, naṭuvunili, vekuḷi eṉṉum oṉpatu cuvikaḷ naṭippuk kuriyavi. (cilappatikāram, kāti 3, uri, pak. 83)  naṭippavar ivaṟṟi naṉku pulappaṭutti naṭikka vēṇṭum. cōmpalukku uriya naṭippu, perumikkuriya naṭippu, veṭkattiṟkuriya naṭippu, maḻiyil naṉivataṟku uriya naṭippu, paṉiyil talippaṭuvataṟ kuriya naṭippu, veyilil celvataṟku uriya naṭippu eṉṟu naṭippu 24 vakiyākap pakuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ovvoru vakikkum ilakkaṇam uṇṭu. (cilappatikāram, kāti 3, uri, pak. 85 - 87)
ki naṭippukaḷil oru kiyiṉāl naṭittuk kāṭṭutal, iru kikaḷiṉāl naṭittuk kāṭṭutal eṉṉum pirivukaḷ uṇṭu. oru kiyiṉāl naṭittuk kāṭṭum naṭippukaḷ 33 vakippaṭum. (cilappatikāram, kāti 3, uri, pak. 92 - 96) avi patāki, iḷampiṟi, viṟpiṭi, kuṭaṅki, valampuri eṉat taṉittaṉip peyarkaḷ peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. inta naṭippukaḷil kiviralkaḷ muṭaṅkutal, nimirtal, kuṉital, virital, toṭutal, viṭutal muṟikaḷil iyakkik kāṭṭappaṭum. iraṇṭu kikaḷāl naṭittuk kāṭṭum naṭippukaḷ 15 vakippaṭum. avi añcali, puṭpāñcali, apaya attam, makaram eṉap pala peyarkaḷip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. (cilappatikāram, kāti 3, uri, pak. 97 - 98)
naṭaṉa makaḷi ak kaliyil tayārikkum āṭal āciriyaṉ amiti, iciyāciriyaṉ amiti, taṇṇumi āciriyaṉ amiti, kuḻalōṉ amiti, yāḻāciriyaṉ amiti eṉpaṉa yāvi eṉpatic cilappatikāram - araṅkēṟṟu kātiyil teḷivuṟak kāṇalām. iṉṉiṉṉavaṟṟil muḻup payiṟci peṟṟavarē iṉṉiṉṉa āciriyar eṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟutalik kāṇa avar kālattil tamiḻakattil iciyum kūttum mikavuyariya niliyil cīrum ciṟappum peṟṟiruntaṉa eṉpatu veḷḷiṭi malipōl viḷakkamākum. āṭaṟkuriya araṅku eṅṅaṉam amikkappaṭal vēṇṭum eṉpatu in nūluḷ teḷivāka viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu; araṅkiṟ pukuntu āṭum iyalpum vivarikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
araṅkiṉiṟ pukuntu mātavi pāṭiṉāḷ; āṭiṉāḷ. appoḻutu mēṟcoṉṉa palavaki āciriyarkaḷ iruntu tattam toḻil ceytaṉar. avvamiyam,
	kuḻalvaḻiniṉṟatuyāḻē;yāḻvaḻittaṇṇuminiṉṟatutakavē;taṇṇumippiṉvaḻiniṉṟatumuḻavē;muḻavoṭukūṭiniṉṟicintatāmantiriki.....
