mātavi āṭiṉāḷ. (araṅkēṟṟu kāti,vari139-146) ikkaruttuṭaṉ, 	neytiraḷnarampiltantamaḻaliyiṉiyaṉṟapāṭaltivarumakaravīṇataṇṇumataḻuvittūṅkak
kivaḻinayaṉamcellakkaṇvaḻimaṉamumcella
aiyanuṇiṭiyahrāṭumnāṭakaaraṅkukaṇṭār.”
eṉa varum (ki.pi. 12- ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟpāṭappaṭṭa) kamparāmāyaṇac ceyyuḷiṉkaruttuoṉṟupaṭalkāṇka.mātavi āṭiya patiṉorāṭal
1. civaperumāṉ viṭṭa ampiṉāl avuṇar ventu vīḻntaṉar. avviṭattil veṇpaṉik kuviyākiya pārati yaraṅkattilē umiyavaḷ oru kūṟṟiṉaḷāy niṉṟu āṭiya koṭu koṭṭi āṭal.
2. vāṉōrākiya tēril nāṉmaṟik kaṭumpari pūṭṭi neṭumpuṟam maṟittu, vārtukil muṭittuk kūrmuḷ piṭittut tērmuṉ niṉṟa ticimukaṉ kāṇumpaṭi pārati vaṭivākiya iṟivaṉ veṇṇīṟṟi aṇintāṭiya pāṇṭaraṅkak kūttu.
3. kaṇṇaṉ āṭiya āṭal pattuḷ kañcaṉvañcaṉiyāl vantayāṉiyiṉ kāṭṭi oṭippataṟku niṉṟaṭiyaalliyattehkutieṉṉumkūttu.4. vāṇaṉākiya avuṇaṉi vellaṟku avaṉi aṟikūvi aḻittu avaṉic cērnta aḷavil uyirpōka nerittut tolinta mallāṭal.
5. kaṭaliṉ naṭuvu niṉṟa cūraṉatu vēṟṟuruvākiya vañcatti yaṟintu avaṉ pōrik kaṭanta murukaṉ akkaṭal naṭuvē tiriyē (aliyē) araṅkamāka niṉṟu tuṭikoṭṭi yāṭiya tuṭikkūttu.
6. avuṇar tām pōr ceytaṟku eṭutta paṭik kalaṅkaḷip pōriṟku āṟṟātu pōṭṭu varuttamuṟṟa aḷavilē murukaṉ taṉ kuṭiyi muṉṉē cāyttu niṉṟāṭiya kuṭik kūttu.
7. kāmaṉ makaṉ aniruttaṉit taṉ makaḷ uḻi kāraṇamāka vāṇaṉ ciṟi vittaliṉ, avaṉuṭiya cō eṉṉum nakara vītiyiṟ ceṉṟu kaṇṇaṉ kuṭaṅ koṇṭāṭiya kuṭak kūttu.
8. āṇmit taṉmi tirinta peṇmik kōlattōṭu kāmaṉ āṭiya pēṭi eṉṉum āṭal.
9. māyōṉi uṇmip pōrāl vellal āṟṟāta avuṇar pāmpu, tēḷ mutaliyaṉa vāyppukutali uṇarntu avaṉ avaṟṟi uḻakkik kaḷitaṟku marakkāl koṇṭu āṭiya marakkālāṭal.
10, avuṇar pōrk kōlattilirukkum poḻutu taṉṉik kaṇṭu mōkittu viḻumpaṭi kollippāvi vaṭivāyc ceyyōḷākiya tirumakaḷāl āṭappaṭṭa pāvi eṉṉum āṭal.
11. vāṇaṉuṭiya periya nakaril vaṭakku vāyilil uḷḷa vayaliṭattē niṉṟu ayirāṇi eṉpavaḷ āṭiya kaṭiyam eṉṉum āṭal. (mā.rā. iḷaṅkōvaṉ, kaliyum vāḻvum, pak. 106 - 107)
mēṟcoṉṉa kūttu vakikaḷuḷ cākkik kūttu eṉpatu oṉṟu. cākkiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ kūttu nikaḻttum marapiṉaṉ. avaṉāl āṭappaṭum kūttu cākkik kūttu eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. cākkiyaṉ oruvaṉ cēraṉ- ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukku muṉ civaperumāṉ āṭiya koṭṭic cētam eṉṉum kūtti āṭiṉaṉ eṉṟu cilappatikāram ceppukiṟatu. (kāti 28, vari 65-79) ic cākkik kūttar tamiḻakattil piṟpaṭṭa nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷilum iruntu vantaṉar eṉpatu cōḻarkālak kalveṭṭuk kaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.
caṅka kālattil irunta kūtta nūlkaḷ
mēṟcollappaṭṭa palvakik kūttu vakikaḷiyum, viṟaliyar-kūttar mutaliyavar iruntamiyiyum nōkkac caṅkakālattil kūtta nūlkaḷ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu veḷḷiṭimali. āyiṉ annūṟ peyarkaḷi uḷḷavāṟu aṟiyac cāṉṟilli. ki.pi. 14-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarum cilap patikārattiṟku uri vakuttavarumāṉa aṭiyārkku nallār, tamakkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ pāṭappaṭṭa cilappatikārat tiṟku uri kāṇkiyil cila kūtta nūṟ peyarkaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟār. avaṟṟuḷ pala cilappatikāra kālattil iruntaṉa eṉṟu koḷvativiṭa, aṭiyārkku nallār kālattil iruntaṉa- avar avaṟṟi naṉku paṭittaṟintavar eṉak kōṭalē poruttamuṭiyatākum. avar kuṟikkum kūtta nūlkaḷāvaṉa:
(1) paratam, (2) akattiyam, (3) muṟuval, (4) cayantam, (5) kuṇanūl, (6) ceyiṟṟiyam, (7) ici nuṇukkam, (8) intirakāsiyam, (9) pañca marapu, (10) parata cēṉāpatīyam, 
(11) mativāṇaṉār nāṭakat tamiḻ nūl, (12) kūtta nūl.
ivaṟṟuḷ mutal iraṇṭu nūlkaḷ aṭiyārkku nallār kālattilēyē iṟantaṉa eṉpatu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. piṟa nūlkaḷiliruntu iraṇṭoru cūttiraṅkaḷ aṭiyārkku nallār, pērāciriyar mutaliyōrāl āḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. in nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟēṉum ik kālattil kiṭittilatu.
pallavar kālam
ciṟpac cāṉṟukaḷ
iṅṅaṉam caṅka kālattiṟ ciṟappuṟṟirunta kūttak kali, pallavar kālattil vaḷarcci peṟṟateṉṟu kūṟalil taṭiyilli. mutalām makēntiraṉ kali iṉpaṉ. avaṉ kūttak kaliyilum peritum īṭupāṭuṭiyavaṉ eṉpati uṟutippaṭuttac cittaṉṉavācal naṭaṉa mātar ōviyaṅkaḷē ēṟṟa cāṉṟākum. avaṉ iyaṟṟiya matta vilācap pirakacaṉam eṉṉum vēṭikki nāṭaka nūlil, civaṉār āṭiya tāṇṭava naṭaṉam mūvulakaṅkaḷiyum orumippaṭutta vallatu eṉṟu pārāṭṭiyuḷḷāṉ. irācacimmaṉ naṭaṉak kaliyil mikka īṭupāṭu koṇṭavaṉ. avaṉ tāṉ kaṭṭuvitta kayilācanātar kōvilil civaṉār āṭiya patāki naṭaṉam, latāvirucika naṭaṉam, ūrttuva tāṇṭavam, āṉanta tāṇṭavam mutaliya naṭaṉa vakikaḷic ciṟpa amippiṟ kāṭṭi aḻiyāp pukaḻ peṟṟāṉ. palavaki naṭikiyar uruvaṅkaḷum ak kōvilil cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kāñci vikuntap perumāḷ kōvilil kūttaṉ, kūttiyar uruvaṅkaḷ iru ciṟpaṅkaḷil cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṭavarum peṇṭirum cērntu āṭiya kūttu vakikaḷum ak kālattil iruntaṉa eṉpataṟku ac ciṟpaṅkaḷ cāṉṟākum.
ilakkiyac cāṉṟu
pallavar kālattil eḻunta civat tirumuṟikaḷil iṟivaṉāṭiya palavakik kūttukkaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. naṭaṉa mātar pāṭal peṟṟa ciṟanta kōvilkaḷil iruntu naṭaṉak kaliyi vaḷarttu vantaṉar eṉpatit tiruppatikaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇalām:
	 tēṉār moḻiyār tiḷittaṅ kāṭit
		tikaḻum kuṭamūkkil
	 valam vanta maṭavārkaḷ naṭamāṭa
		muḻavatira maḻiyeṉ ṟañcic
	cilamanti alamantu maramēṟi
		mukilpārkkum tiruviyāṟē
	 taṇṭu uṭukki tāḷam takki cāra
		naṭam payilpavar uṟiyum pukār
	 cīrālē pāṭal āṭal citivillatōr
		ērārpūṅ kacci
eṉpaṉavum, ivi pōṉṟu tiruppatikaṅkaḷil varuvaṉavum ak kālak kūttukkali vaḷarcciyi naṉku uṇarttu vaṉavākum. ki.pi. 9-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta cuntarari maṇantu koṇṭa paraviyār tiruvārūrk kōvil naṭaṉa mātarāvar. avar āṭal- pāṭalkaḷiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉār. nāyaṉmār kālamākiya pallavar kālattil tillip perumāṉ kūttapperumāṉ(naṭarācar) eṉṟu nāyaṉmārāṟ pāṭap paṭṭāṉ. avaṉatu tirukkūṭattic ciṟappittu avarkaḷ pala pākkaḷ pāṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. pallavar kālattuk kōvil kaḷil naṭaṉa mātar iruntaṉar eṉpataṟku mēṟcoṉṉa ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷē yaṉṟik kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷum uṇṭu. am mātaracikaḷē iciyiyum kūttiyum vaḷarttaṉar. avarkaḷ ak kālattil kaṇikiyar, māṇikkattār eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kāñci- muttīccurar kōvilil maṭṭum nāṟpattiraṇṭu pēr iruntu ici, kūttu ākiya kalikaḷi vaḷarttaṉar eṉiṉ, piṟa kōvilkaḷil iruntārip paṟṟik kūṟavum vēṇṭumō? atu paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ virikkiṟ perukum.
cōḻar kālam
ciṟpaṅkaḷ
tamiḻaka varalāṟṟil cōḻar kālam (900-1300) poṟkālam eṉṉalām. akkālattil civa- viṇava camayaṅkaḷ naṉku vaḷarcci peṟṟaṉa. kōvilkaḷ kaṟkōvilkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. ōviyam- ciṟpam- ici- naṭaṉam- nāṭakam mutaliya kalikaḷ naṉku vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa. cōḻarkaḷ ciṟanta moḻip
paṟṟum kaliyuṇarcciyum koṇṭavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ kālattil putuppikkap paṭṭaṉavum putiyaṉavākak kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉavumāṉa kōvilkaḷil ellām maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṉ - karuvaṟikaḷiṉ aṭippakutikaḷil ellām, naṭaṉa vakikaḷ cetukkappaṭṭaṉa. citamparam, kāñci, tañci, kaṅkikoṇṭa cōḻapuram, tiruvaṇṇāmali mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa periya kōvil kaḷik kūrntu kavaṉittu ivvuṇmiyi aṟiyalām. kūttiyar maṭṭumē taṉittiruntu āṭutal mutaliya āṭal vakikaḷ - kaṇakkiṭaṟkariya (mikap palavākiya) naṭaṉa vakikaḷ- ciṟpaṅ kaḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ōviyac cāṉṟu
tañcip periyakōvil karuvaṟiyiṉ puṟaccuvaril cōḻar kāla aḻakikaḷ naṭaṉamāṭum ōviyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. avartam kūntal oppaṉi, uṭic ciṟappu, icik karuvikaḷ, mukap polivu, uruva amippu ivi aṉitti yum anta ōviyattil kaṇṭu kaḷikkalām.
kalveṭṭukkaḷ
ciṟanta civapaktaṉāṉa irācarācaṉ, tāṉ tañciyil amitta periya kōvilil ici, naṭaṉam ākiya iraṇṭiyum vaḷarkkap patiyilār nāṉūṟṟuvarip pala kōvilkaḷiliruntu varavaḻittu, ovvoruvarukkum oru vīṭum, oru vēli nilamum aḷittāṉ eṉṟu ap periya kōvil kalveṭṭup pēcukiṟatu. am mātaracikaḷ pala kōvil kaḷiliruntu vantavarkaḷ eṉpati nōkkap pala kōvil kaḷilum kūttiyar iruntaṉar eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṟataṉṟō! tiruviṭimarutūri yaṭutta kāmaracavalli eṉṟa patiyil cākkik kūttil vallavaṉ mutalām irācēntiraṉ kālattil iruntāṉ. avaṉ cākki mārāyaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭam peṟṟa tākak kalveṭṭuk kuṟikkiṉṟatu. eṉavē, cilappatikārattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa cākkik kūttu ki.pi. 11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum tamiḻakattil iruntatu eṉpatu pulaṉākiṟa taṉṟō!
ilakkiyam
cōḻar kālattu ilakkiyaṅkaḷuṭ ciṟantaṉa periya purāṇamum kampa rāmāyaṇamumē eṉṉalām. periya purāṇattil naṭaṉam teḷivākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
	 kaṟpakarpūn taḷiraṭipōyk kāmarucā rikiceyya
uṟpalameṉ mukiḻviralvaṭ ṭaṇiyōṭum kipeyarap
popuṟumak kiyiṉvaḻip poṟruyaṟkaṇ puṭipeyara
aṟputap poṟ koṭituṭaṅki āṭuvapōl āṭuvār.
			                                                              (tirunāvukkaracar purāṇam, 420)
kampar tam kālattu naṭaṉattic ciṟappitta taṉmi muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. cōḻar kālattiṟ ciṟappākak kūṟattakka teṉṉa? naṭaṉa vakikaḷ eṉṟum aḻiyāta muṟiyil kaṟcuvarkaḷiṟ cetukkap peṟṟamiyē ciṟappeṉṉalām. avarkaḷ kālattil civam naṉku vaḷarntatātaliṉ, camayam toṭarpāka naṭaṉamum naṉku vaḷarntateṉpati aiyattiṟ kiṭamiṉṟik kūṟalām.
piṟkālattil
tamiḻakap pakutikaḷ nāyakkarālum, makārāṭṭirarālum āḷappaṭṭa kālattil, karunāṭaka ici tamiḻakattiṟ pukuntatu; parata nāṭṭiyam tali tūkkiyatu. ak kālattil vaṭamoḻi nāṭṭiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻil peyartteḻutap paṭṭaṉa. (parata cātiram - tamiḻil eḻutiyavar arapatta nāvalar. mutal attiyāyam pāvaviyal karalakṣaṇaṅkaḷ yāvum teḷivuṟuttu kiṟatu) avaṟṟuḷ (1) makāpārata cūṭāmaṇi, (2) apinaya tarppaṇam (3) apinaya tarppaṇa vilāsam. (4) paravarāka tāḷam, (5) cuttāṉantap pirakācam, (6) ātimūla paratam, (7) parata cātiram, (8) parata cēṉāpatīyam, (9) capārañcita cintāmaṇi eṉpaṉa kuṟikkattakkavi. ivaṟṟuḷ cila iṉṉum accāka villi.
tēvatāci oḻippuc caṭṭam vantatumutal vaḻivaḻiyāka naṭaṉakkali payiṉṟuvanta marapiṉar ap payiṟciyi viṭṭu viṭṭaṉar. kōvil ūrvalaṅkaḷil atukāṟum naṭaṉamāṭi vanta muṟiyum oḻintatu. cila āṇṭukaḷil naṭaṉak kali iṟantupaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟalām. paṭakkāṭci ciṟanta muṟiyil vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatumutal - ēṟattāḻap patiṉintu āṇṭu kaḷākattāṉ- tamiḻakattil palarum naṭaṉak kaliyip payiṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉar. iṟivaṉ āṭiya naṭaṉa vakikaḷ iṉṟu āṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. kuṟṟālak kuṟavañci mutaliyaṉavum apinayattil kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu ak kaliyil ārva muḷḷa ellāc cātiyiṉarum ak kaliyip payilukiṉṟaṉar. itu varavēṟkattakka vaḷarcciyākum.

8. nāṭakak kali
caṅka kālattil
kūttu eṉṉum col mutalil naṭaṉattiyum, piṉpu kati taḻuvi varum kūttākiya nāṭakattiyum kuṟittatu. iyaṟṟamiḻip pulavarum, icit tamiḻip pāṇarum pēṇi vaḷarttāṟ pōlavē, naṭaṉattiyum nāṭakattiyum kūttar eṉpōr pēṇi vaḷarttaṉar. naṭaṉam āṭum makaḷir viṟaliyar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. uḷḷak kuṟippup puṟattil tōṉṟumpaṭi tiṟampaṭa naṭippavaḷ viṟali (tiruccirāpaḷḷikku aṇmiyil uḷḷa viṟali mali eṉpatu iṉṟu tavaṟāka viṟālimali eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu) eṉappaṭṭāḷ. kūtti,kūttar ākiya ivarkaḷ kati taḻuvi varum kūttukkaḷi āṭiṉar. aṅṅaṉam āṭiya poḻutu, āṇmakaṉ porunaṉ eṉṟum peyar peṟṟāṉ.
tamiḻ toṉṟutoṭṭu iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum mūṉṟu pirivukaḷip peṟṟiruntatu. cilappatikāram oṉṟē iṉṟu nāṭakak kāppiyamāka iruntu varukiṟatu. cilappatikāra kālattil vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kūṭṭuṟavu tamiḻakattil mikutiyāka iruntatu. ak kālattil nāṭakam eṉṉum col, kūttu eṉṉum col pōṉṟē naṭaṉattiyum, kati taḻuvi varum kūttiyum kuṟittatu. nāṭakak kāppiya naṉṉūl eṉa varum toṭaril uḷḷa nāṭakam eṉṉum collukkuk kati taḻuvi varum kūttu eṉṟu poruḷ eḻutiyiruttal kavaṉikkat takkatu. eṉavē, nāṭakam paṟṟiya kāviyaṅkaḷ maṇimēkali āciriyar kālamākiya ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iruntaṉa eṉpatu teḷivu. maṇimēkalikku muṟpaṭṭa tiruk kuṟaḷilum kūttāṭṭavi kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅkuk kūttāṭutal eṉpatu naṭittal eṉṉum poruḷil vantuḷḷatu.
kūttu allatu nāṭakam eṉpatu nuṇkalikaḷuḷ oṉṟākum. veḷināṭukaḷuṭaṉ paṉṉeṭuṅ kālamāka vāṇikam ceytuvanta tamiḻar- iyal, icik kalikaḷil vallava rāyirunta tamiḻar- nāṭakak kaliyilum nallatērcci peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷutal tavaṟākātu. iyal, ici eṉṉum iraṇṭu pirivukaḷ kēṭpōrukku iṉpam taruvaṉa. nāṭakam kēḷvi iṉpattōṭu, kāṭciyiṉpamum payappatākum eṉavē, nāṭakamē mikka payaḷaḷḷatāka aṟivuṭiyōr karutuvar. nāṭakattil iyal, ici ākiya iraṇṭum kalantuḷḷaṉa. nāṭakattilēyē muttamiḻiyum oruṅkē kāṇalām.
kōvil viḻākkaḷilēyē nāṭakam tōṟṟam eṭuttatu eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu. āṭal, pāṭal eṉṉum iraṇṭiṉ cērkkiyāka mutalil nāṭakam amintiruntatu. piṉpu, pāṭṭālaminta urinaṭi iṭiyiṭiyē kalantatu. ataṉ piṟaku pēccu naṭiyilaminta urinaṭi cērntatu. eṉavē, āṭal, pāṭal vaṭivil aminta urinaṭi, pēccu urinaṭi eṉpaṉa cērntu nāṭakatti aḻaku ceytaṉa. iṅṅaṉam vaḷarat talippaṭṭa nāṭakam, aracarkku eṉṟum, potumakkaḷukku eṉṟum iruvakiyākap pirintatu. avi, vēttiyal, potuviyal eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
nāṭakam naṉmuṟiyil vaḷarntuvanta poḻutu, in nāṭṭil vantu taṅkic celvākkup peṟṟa āriyarum, camaṇarum nāṭakam kāmatti mikutippaṭuttuva teṉṟu tavaṟāka eṇṇiṉar; ataṉāl, tām ceyta nūlkaḷil nāṭakattiṉ matippik kuṟittaṉar.; avarkaḷ celvākku mikutip paṭṭirunta kālattil, nāṭakat tamiḻi vaḷara voṭṭāmal taṭuttaṉar. eṉavē, nāṭaka vaḷarcci paṭippaṭiyākak kuṟintatu. (vi.kō.cū. tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ varalāṟu, pak.45)
pallavar kālattil
ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil pallava makēntiravarmaṉ, matta vilācap pirakacaṉam eṉṉum vēṭikki nāṭakatti vaṭamoḻi yil eḻutiṉāṉ. (pallavar varalāṟu, pak. 109) mēlum, vaṭa moḻiyil ciṟu nāṭakaṅkaḷ cila irācacimma pallavaṉ kālattil ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ( pallavar varalāṟu, pak.165)  pakti iyakkam paravat toṭaṅkiya akkālattil camayat toṭarpāṉa nāṭakaṅkaḷ talitūkkiṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ki.pi. 8-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ceyyappeṟṟa utayaṇaṉ varalāṟṟik kūṟum peruṅkati yilum nāṭakattip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ cila kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
	nayattiṟam porunta nāṭakam kaṇṭum 
	naṇpuṇat teḷitta nāṭakam pōla
	vāyiṟ kūttum cērip pāṭalum
	kōyil nāṭakak kuḻukkaḷum varukeṉa
kōyil nāṭakakkuḻu- araṇmaṉiyil naṭippōr kūṭṭam eṉavarum patippāciriyar aṭikkuṟippuk kāṇattakum. ki.pi. 8-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻakattil nāṭakam naṭikkappaṭṭatiyum, nāṭakak kuḻuviṉar iruntamiyiyum iv varikaḷ terivikkiṉṟaṉa allavā?
ki.pi. 9-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta māṇikka vācakar tamatu tiruvācakattil, nāṭakattāl uṉṉaṭiyār pōl naṭittu eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttalālum, nammāḻvār, piṟavimā māyak kūttiṉiyē eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttalālum, ki.pi. 9-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil  nāṭakaṅkaḷ naṭittuk kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpati naṉkaṟiyalām.
cōḻar kālattil
ki.pi. 10-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya cīvaka cintāmaṇi, nāṭakam kāmatti mikuvikkiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷatu kāṇattakum:
	 iḷimiyaṅ kaḻaṉic cāyam ēṟuḻu teripoṉ vēli
vaḷimuyaṅ kuruva meṉṟōḷ varampupōy vaṉappu vittik
kiḷinaram(pu) iciyum kūttum kēḻtteḻun tīṉṟa kāma
viḷipayaṉ iṉitiṉ tuyttu vīṇivēn(tu) uṟiyu mātō.						 (ce. 2398)
nāṭakatti virumpik kāṇpava rkaṇkaḷit tōṇṭiyum..............ivvāṟupiṟara aimpoṟiyālnukarāmaltaṭuttuyāmumnukarcciyakkiviṭṭēmeṉavarumtoṭar, camaṇar nāṭakatti enta aḷavu veṟuttaṉar eṉpati naṉku kāṭṭavallatu.	nāṭakam nayantu kāṇpār nalaṅkiḷar kaṇkaḷ cūṉṟum-						-mutti ilampakam, 2989
ivaṟṟāl, cintāmaṇi eḻutappeṟṟa ki.pi. 10-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil nāṭakaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil naṭikkappeṟṟaṉa eṉṉum uṇmiyi uṇaralām.
piṟkālac cōḻar kālattil āṇṭutōṟum vikāci viḻāvil, tañci irācarācēcuvarattil, irācarācēcuvara nāṭakam naṭittuk kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. ataṉi naṭittuk kāṭṭiya vicayarācēntira āccāriyaṉukku āṇṭutōṟum 120 kalam bnaljrappaṭṭatu.(S.I.I.II. 67)  irācarācaṉ tañcāvūril periya kōvilik kaṭṭiya muṟi, avaṉatu varalāṟu, avaṉ maṉiviyar ak kōvilukku aḷitta nivantaṅkaḷ, ak kōvilip paṟṟik karuvūrttēvar pāṭiyatu pōṉṟa pala ceytikaḷ in nāṭakattil pala kāṭcikaḷāka amintirukkalām.
vikkiramātitta āccāriyaṉ eṉṟa irācarāca nāṭakap piriyaṉ eṉpavaṉ pantaṇi nallūril naṭṭuvappaṅku, meymmaṭṭippaṅku (nāṭakakkāṇi) ivaṟṟip peṟṟavaṉāy iruntāṉ eṉṟu avvūrk kalveṭṭuk kūṟuvatāl, irācarāca nāṭakam (mutalām irācarācaṉip paṟṟiyatu) eṉa oṉṟu iruntatu, an nāṭakam naṭikkappaṭṭatu eṉpavaṟṟi aṟiyalām. (tamiḻppoḻil, 23, pak.152-3) innūlil irāca rācaṉuṭiya iḷamipparuvam, avaṉ aracaṉāṉami, pōrc ceytikaḷ, āṭcimuṟi, irācarācēcuvaram eṭuppittami, nampiyik koṇṭu tirumuṟikaḷ tokuttami mutaliya ceytikaḷ pala kāṭcikaḷāka iṭam peṟṟirukkalām.
mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattil pūmpuliyūr nāṭakam eṉṟa oṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭatu. ataṉic ceytavaṉukkup paricu tarappaṭṭatu. (129 of 1902) atu tiruppātirip puliyūrip paṟṟiyatu; ammaṉ kaṉṉikiyāyiruntu civaṉi vaḻipaṭṭatu, appar camaṇarāyiruntami, piṉpu civarāṉami, camaṇa ruṭiya koṭumikaḷukku āḷāṉami, piṟaku kaṭalil mitantu kari cērntami, tiruvatikik kōvilil patikam pāṭiṉami, makēntiraṉ aṅkirunta camaṇap paḷḷiyi aḻittuk kuṇapara īcuvaram kaṭṭiṉami pōṉṟavaṟṟik kāṭcikaḷākak koṇṭa nūlāka irukkalām. atu naṭikkap peṟṟamikkuc cāṉṟu illiyāyiṉum, camayappaṟṟu mikkirunta ak kālattil atu naṭikkappaṭṭateṉak karututal tavaṟākātu. iṅṅaṉam nāyaṉmārkaḷiyum aracarkaḷiyum paṟṟiya nāṭakaṅkaḷ cila vēṉum ak kālattil naṭikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu koḷḷalām.
cōḻarkkup piṉ
ki.pi. 14-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil,  mālik-kāpūr paṭiyeṭuppukkup piṟaku cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya aracukaḷ nili taḷarntaṉa. vicayanakara vēntar āṭci ciṟitukālam camayattip pātukāttatu. appoḻutu ici, naṭaṉam, nāṭakam mutaliya kalikaḷ puttuyir peṟṟaṉa. teṉṉāṭṭil nāyakkarāṭci maṟiyum vari ik kalikaḷ ōraḷavu uyirpeṟṟu vāḻntaṉa. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṟaku nāṭu pala tuṟikaḷilum allaṟpaṭṭa kāraṇattāl nāṭakam mutaliya kalikaḷ kavaṉippāraṟṟuk kiṭantaṉa.
ki.pi. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutitoṭṭuk kūttu nūlkaḷ cila, vēraṟṟu vīḻnta nāṭakat tamiḻiṉiṉṟum kiḷip paṉavāyiṉa. iṭiyiṭiyē kavikūṟṟu mēvi iḻiciṉar naṭakkum iyalpiṉavākik kūttum pāṭṭum koṇṭu naṭappaṉa ellām kūttu nūlkaḷām. cīkāḻi aruṇācalak kavirāyar ceyta irāma nāṭakam, kumarakurupara cuvāmikaḷ ceyta mīṉāṭciyammi kuṟam, tirukūṭarācappak kavirāyar ceyta kuṟṟālak kuṟavañci eṉpaṉa ik kūttu nūliṉ pāṟpaṭuvaṉavām. mukkūṭaṟpaḷḷu, paṟāḷi vināyakar paḷḷu mutaliyaṉavum kūttu nūlkaḷēyām. ivi ellām iyaṟṟamiḻp pulami cāṉṟapāvalar iyaṟṟiyaṉavām. cuttāṉantap pirakācam eṉṟa toru parata nūl iṭikkālattiṉ toṭakkattil ēṟpaṭṭatu; veḷippaṭāmalirukkiṉṟatu. piṉpu, ki.pi. 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil irunta arapatta nāvalar eṉpār parata cātiram eṉṟatoru nūl ceytuḷḷār. (vi.kō. N., tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ varalāṟu, pak. 45.)
ki.pi. 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpātiyil koṭṭiyūrc civakkoḻuntu tēcikar tañciyi āṇṭa carapōcimaṉṉar mītu pāṭiya kuṟavañci nāṭakam kuṟippiṭattakkatu. annāṭakam tañcip periya kōvilil naṭikkappaṭṭu vantatu. atē nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ kaṭippakutiyil pērāciriyar cuntarampiḷḷi pāṭiya maṉōṉmaṇīya nāṭakamum pōṟṟattakkatuvē.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil
nām vāḻum ivvirupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpātiyil nāṭakakkali naṉku vaḷarntatu. pammal campanta mutaliyār avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa pala nāṭakaṅkaḷ nāṭeṅkum naṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟanta nāṭaka āciriyarāṉa caṅkaratācu cuvāmikaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa nāṭakaṅkaḷ palavākum. avaṟṟuḷ, apimaṉyu cuntari, pārvati kalyāṇam, pirapuliṅka līli, vaḷḷi tirumaṇam, pātukā paṭṭāpiṣēkam, ilaṅkā takaṉam, alli yarccuṉā, ciṟuttoṇṭar, cati aṉucūyā, pavaḷakkoṭi, maṇimēkali, miruccakaṭi, cīmantaṉi, cāvittiri, kōvalaṉ, pirakalātaṉ eṉpaṉa kuṟikkattakkavi. kaṇṇiya nāyuṭu nāṭakak kuḻuviṉar naṭittu vanta kiruṣṇalīli, tacāvatāram, āṇṭāḷ mutaliya nāṭakaṅkaḷ muppatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ nāṭṭil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa.
in nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpātiyil caṅkaratācu cuvāmikaḷ iṇiyaṟṟa nāṭaka āciriyarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. ivartam māṇavarkaḷ tamiḻakam muḻutum paraviyuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ āṅkāṅku iruntukoṇṭu tammāl iyalumaḷavu ik kaliyi vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ vaḷarnta maturiyil ivartam māṇavarkaḷ caṅkaṅkaḷi amittu nāṭakap payiṟci aḷittu varukiṉṟaṉar; maturi, irāmanātapuram, tirucci māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil nāṭakaṅkaḷi naṭattukiṉṟaṉar.
caṅkaratācu cuvāmikaḷiṉ māṇavarkaḷākiya ṭi.kē. caṇmukam cakōtararkaḷ iṉṟiya nāṭakat tuṟiyilum naṭippukkaliyilum ciṟanta viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ak kalikku ēṟṟa oḻukkamum avarkaḷpāl amintuḷḷami kuṟippiṭattakum. avarkaḷ naṭittuvarum nāṭakaṅkaḷuḷ auviyār, maṉitaṉ, iṉpekṭar, irācarāca cōḻaṉ eṉpaṉa kuṟikkat takkavi. ivaṟṟuḷḷum irācarāca cōḻaṉ iṇiyaṟṟa nāṭakamākum. kāṇpavar uḷḷaṅkaḷik koḷḷi koḷḷum mikacciṟanta nāṭakam eṉṟu itaṉik kūṟalām. cōḻar varalāṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu variyap peṟṟuḷḷa in nāṭakam, makkaḷukku varalāṟṟu uṇarcciyiyum paktiyiyum oḻukkattiyum oruṅkē ūṭṭavallatu.
navāp irācamāṇikkattiṉ kuḻuviṉar vaḷḷi tirumaṇam, campūrṇa rāmāyaṇam mutaliya nāṭakaṅkaḷi naṭittu vantaṉar.
kālattiṟkēṟṟa cīrtiruttaṅkaḷik koṇṭu nāṭakaṅkaḷ pala ippoḻutu palarāl naṭikkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. eṉ.e. kiruṣṇaṉ kuḻuviṉar, em.ji.irāmaccantiraṉ kuḻuviṉar, e. e. irācēntiraṉ kuḻuviṉar, kē.ār. irāmacāmi kuḻuviṉar, civājikaṇēcaṉ kuḻuviṉar, em. ār.irātā kuḻuviṉar, cakaranāmam kuḻuviṉar, kē. ē. taṅkavēlu kuḻuviṉar mutaliyōr payaṉtaraktakka nāṭakaṅkaḷi naṭatti vantaṉar. avaruṭ cilar iṉṟum naṭatti varukiṉṟaṉar.
i.ār. cakātēvaṉ kuḻuviṉar pulittēvaṉ nāṭakattic ciṟanta muṟiyil an nāḷil naṭatti vantaṉar. pulittēvaṉē veḷḷiyari etirtta mutal tamiḻ vīraṉ eṉpatum, avaṉatu civapakti, vīram, oḻukkam mutaliyaṉavum an nāṭakattil naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. an nāṭakattil ciṟanta tamiḻnaṭi pēcappaṭṭatu. annāṭaka nūli arumpāṭupaṭṭu eḻutiya kalkaṇṭu āciriyar tamiḻvāṇaṉ avarkaṭkut tamiḻakam naṉṟi pārāṭṭak kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tamiḻakattil pallāyiram iḷiñarkaḷi nalla tamiḻil pēcap paḻakkivanta aṟiñar aṇṇāturi, cantiramōkaṉ, nītitēvaṉ mayakkam, ōr iravu, vēlikkāri, corkkavācal mutaliya nāṭakaṅkaḷi eḻutiyuḷḷār. avaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟi, pammal campanta mutaliyār pōlavē, nūlāciriyarē naṭippatu vaḻakkam. pēccuk kaliyiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatu pōṉṟē, aṇṇāturi naṭippuk kaliyilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉār. itu pōṉṟē mu. karuṇānitiyum ciṟanta nāṭaka āciriyarākavum, naṭikarākavum viḷaṅkukiṟār.
srītēvi nāṭaka capāviṉ urimiyāḷarāṉa kē. eṉ irattiṉam kuḻuviṉar pala nāṭakaṅkaḷi naṭatti vantaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ nantivarmaṉ eṉpatum oṉṟu. teḷḷāṟeṟinta nantivarmaṉ varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟavaṉ; ciṟanta pōrvīraṉ; mikacciṟanta civapaktaṉ; nantikkalampakam pāṭappeṟṟavaṉ. ap perumakaṉip paṟṟiya nāṭakam mikavum naṉmuṟiyil naṭikkappeṟṟatu.
itukāṟum kūṟappeṟṟa nāṭakak kuḻuviṉarēyallāmal, aracāṅka aluvalkaḷilum, piṟa tuṟikaḷilum vēli pārppavarkaḷ pērūrkaḷil ellām capikaḷi amittu nāṭakaṅkaḷi naṭittu varukiṉṟaṉar. itu potumakkaḷiṭam enta aḷavu nāṭakap paṟṟuḷḷatu eṉpati naṉku uṇarttukiṟatu.
mattiya aracāṅkam, cakaranāmam eṉṟa naṭikar poṟuppil naṭikarkkuriya naṭikar payiṟcippaḷḷi oṉṟic ceṉṉiyil toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu. aracāṅkam nāṭakak kali vaḷarcciyil nallārvam kāṭṭat toṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu eṉpataṟku itu ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. nam ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattilum, ik kali vaḷarccikku vakuppu naṭattavum, ataṉil tēṟiyavarkkup paṭṭam vaḻaṅkavum ēṟpāṭu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
muṭivuri
tamiḻ naṭikar, tamiḻakattu varalāṟṟiyum ilakkiyattiyum payilutal nallatu. tūya, eḷiya tamiḻnaṭiyil, uriyāṭalkaḷi amittu naṭittal varavēṟkat takkatu. pāṭalkaḷ cilavākavum uriyāṭalkaḷ palavākavum amintuḷḷa nāṭakaṅkaḷiyē naṭittal ēṟpuṭiyatu. poruttamaṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil ellām pāṭutal nāṭakac cuviyik keṭuttuviṭum. ivi aṉittiṟkum mēlāka naṭikariṭam kaṭṭuppāṭum oḻukkamum mikuntiruttal vēṇṭum. varuṅkālat tamiḻakattil paṭṭampeṟṟa iḷiñarkaḷum ik kaliyil payiṟci peṟutal nallatu. kaṟṟavar, nāṭakattil naṭippatu varavēṟkattakkatu. tamiḻuṇarcci vīṟu koṇṭuḷḷa ik kālattil, tamiḻ naṭikar nāṭakakkali vaḷarcciyil ūkkam koḷḷutal nallatu. naṉmuṟiyil amiyum nāṭakaṅkaḷit tamiḻ makkaḷ eppoḻutum varavēṟpar eṉpatu tiṇṇam.
vāṉoliyilum nāṭakaṅkaḷ avvappōtu oliparappap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ik kāla uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷilum kallūrikaḷilum nāṭaka maṉṟaṅkaḷ iyaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ cārpil iḷiñarkaḷ naṭipputtiṟami peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu makiḻccikkuriya ceytiyākum. māṇavarōṭu āciriyarum orō vaḻi naṭittal pārāṭṭattakuvatām.

9. maruttuvak kali
caṅka kālattil
caṅka kālattil maruttuvak kaliyip paṟṟiya vivaraṅ kaḷi aṟiyattakka cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭikkavilli. āyiṉum maruttuvaṉ tāmōtaraṉār eṉṟa pulavar oruvar ak kālattil iruntār eṉpati avar pāṭalkoṇṭu aṟikiṉṟōm. avaratu maruttuvaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭattiṉāl, maruntukaḷ uṇmiyum maruttuvak kaliyil tāmōtaraṉār paṇpaṭṭavar eṉpatiyum nām aṟital kūṭum. tirukkuṟaḷil maruntu eṉṉum atikāram iruppati nōkka, akkālattil maruttuvak kali tamiḻakattil paraviyiruntatueṉpatum maruttuvar palar iruntaṉar eṉpatum teḷiyalām. paṇṇaṉ pacippiṇi maruttuvaṉ eṉṟa peyar peṟṟāṉ. it toṭar nōy nīkkum maruttuvaṉ uṇmiyip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟatu.
tirukkuṟaḷil maruttuvam
oruvaṉ uṭalukku ottapaṭi uṇavum ceyalkaḷum iruttal vēṇṭum. ivi ovvātu mikutippaṭiṉum allatu kuṟiyiṉum vātam, pittam, ai eṉṉum mūṉṟu nōyum avaṉukkut tuṉpam ceyyum eṉṟu maruttuva nūlōr kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. oruvaṉ tāṉ uṇṭatu cerittuviṭṭatu eṉpati naṉku aṟinta piṉpē uṇṇa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ceypavaṉukku marunteṉṟu oṉṟu tēviyilli. oruvaṉ taṉakkuc cerikkattakka aḷavaṟintē uṇṇavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam aḷavaṟintu uṇpavaṉ nīṇṭanāḷ vāḻvāṉ. uṭampukku ottukkoḷḷāta uṇavup poruḷkaḷi uṇṇalākātu. uṭampukku ēṟṟa uṇavāyiṉum ciṟitaḷavu kuṟivāka uṇpatu nallatu; avaṉatu iṉpam nīṅkātu niṟkum; aḷavaṟintu uṇṇātavaṉiṭam nōy nīṅkātu nilittu niṟkum.
taṉ cerimāṉa āṟṟaliyum ēṟṟa uṇaviyum uṇṇattakka kālattiyum ārāyātu uṇṇum oruvaṉiṭam nōykaḷ mikappalavāka vaḷarum. maruttuvaṉ mutalil nōyāḷiyiṭam nikaḻkiṉṟa nōyi ataṉ kuṟikaḷāl iṉṉateṉṟu tuṇiyavēṇṭum; piṉ atu varuvataṟku uriya kāraṇatti ārāyntu teḷiya vēṇṭum; piṉpu an nōyi nīkkum vaḻiyi aṟintu avvaḻi piḻiyāmaṟ ceyyavēṇṭum. maruttuva nūlik kaṟṟavaṉ nōyāḷiyiṉ aḷavum (paruvam, vētaṉi, vali ivaṟṟiṉ aḷavum), nōyaḷavum (cāttiyam, acāttiyam, yāppiyam, nāṭpaṭṭa nōy eṉpaṉavum), kālamum (nōyiṉ toṭakkam, naṭu, īṟu eṉṉum ataṉ paruva vēṟupāṭum) ākiya mūṉṟum piḻiyāmal maruttuva nūl neṟiyālum uṇarvu mikutiyālum ārāyntu ceyya vēṇṭum. nōyāḷi, maruttuvaṉ, maruntu, maruntip piḻiyāmal uṭkoḷḷal eṉṟa nāṉkum piṇikku maruntākum. ivi vaḷḷuvar karuttukkaḷ.
pallavar kālattil
pallavar kālattil kallāla maram, karucarāṅkaṇṇi mutaliya maruntu maraṅkaḷiyum ceṭikaḷiyum payiriṭa akkāla makkaḷ vari celuttiṉar eṉṟu kalveṭṭukkaḷ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. cukku, miḷaku, tippili eṉpavi maruntup poruḷkaḷ. ivi mūṉṟiṉālākiya kaṭukam (cērkki) oruvar uṭal nōyi māṟṟi iṉpam puriyum. avi pōlavē mummūṉṟu nītip poruḷkaḷik koṇṭa nūṟu pāṭalkaḷik koṇṭa nūlukku tirikaṭukam eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar.
maruttuva nūliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa kaṇṭaṅkattari vēr, ciṟuvaḻutuṇivēr, ciṟumallivēr, neruñcivēr, perumalli vēr ākiya pañca mūlaṅkaḷ makkaḷ piṇikaḷit tīrkkum. avipōla aintintu nītipporuḷkaḷik koṇṭa nūṟu ceyyuṭkaḷālākiya nūlukkuc ciṟupañca mūlam eṉṟa peyari nam muṉṉōr vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar.
ēlam, ilavaṅkam, ciṟu nāvalpū, miḷaku, tippili, cukku eṉṟa āṟu maruntup poruḷkaḷum oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, āṟu varākaṉ eṭi kalanta poṭi (cūraṇam) ēlāti eṉappaṭum. im maruntip pōlavē uḷanōyit tavirkka avvāṟu poruḷkaḷikkoṇṭa eṇpatu ceyyuṭkaḷi uṭiya nūl ēlāti eṉappaṭṭatu.
ivi patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷuḷ mūṉṟākum. ivvāṟu maruntu vakikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyē nūṟpeyar kaḷāka nam muṉṉōr vittu vaḻaṅkiṉar eṉpati nōkka, avartam maruttuva aṟivi eṉṉeṉṟu kūṟi viyappatu! vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tamiḻakattil vāḻat toṭaṅkiyatu mutal avarkaḷatu āyurvēta maruttuva muṟi tamiḻakattil kāl koṇṭatu.
pallavar kālattil vāḻnta nāyaṉmār varalāṟukaḷil maruttuvam paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. kaṇṇappar civaliṅkattiṉ kaṇṇiliruntu vaṭinta kurutiyi niṟuttap paccilikaḷip piḻintu avaṟṟiṉ cāṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttiṉār eṉṟu varalāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. kaṇṇappar, miṉ ceyvār pakaḻippuṇkaḷ tīrkkumey maruntu tēṭic ceṉṟār;
“puṉattiṭip paṟittuk koṇṭu pūta nāyakaṉpāl vitta
 maṉattiṉuṅ kaṭitu vantam maruntukaḷ piḻintu vārttār”
			       (kaṇṇappar purāṇam, ce.`75-176)
eṉpatu kaṇṇappa nāyaṉār purāṇam.
kolli maḻavaṉ makaḷ muyalakaṉ eṉṟa nōyāl varuntiṉāḷ. maruttuvar palar avaḷatu nōyit tīrkka muyaṉṟaṉar; payaṉilli. iṟutiyil ñāṉacampantar patikam pāṭi annōyi akaṟṟiṉār eṉpatu varalāṟu. (campantar purāṇam, ce.311-320)
tirunāvukkaracarukkuk koṭiya cūlinōy vantapoḻutu, camaṇa muṉivar nīri mantirittuk kuṭikkak koṭuttaṉar; mayiṟ pīliyāl uṭampit taṭaviṉar. (tirunāvukkaracar purāṇam, ce.53) iṅṅaṉam maruttuvattil mantiritta nīrik kuṭippittalum mayiṟpīliyāl uṭampit taṭavutalum iṉṟum uḷḷa vaḻakkēyām.
ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta niṉṟacīr neṭumāṟaṉ eṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉukku veppu nōy kaṇṭatu. uṭaṉē maturiyil irunta maruttuvar palar tām kaṟṟa maruttuva nūl kaḷiṉpaṭi veppu nōykkuriya maruntukaḷik koṭuttaṉar eṉṟu periyapurāṇam pēcukiṟatu.
 	maruttuvanūlavartaṅkaḷpalkalikaḷilvakutta
tiruttakuntoḻilyāviyumceytaṉar.....
			 (campantar purāṇam, ce. 713)
cōḻar kālattil
piṟkālac cōḻar kālattil cēkkiḻār piṟanta kuṉṟattūril orumaruttuvaṉ iruntāṉ eṉṟu kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. akkālattil vāḻnta cēkkiḻār tamatu periyapurāṇattil maruntu vivaraṅkaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟār. ikkuṟippu, pallavar kāla maruntu vakikaḷum maruntu muṟikaḷum toṭarntu cōḻar kālattilum iruntaṉa eṉpati uṇarttuvatākum.cila kōvilkaḷi aṭuttu maruttuva maṉikaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟil iruvaki maruttuvar iruntaṉar. avaruḷ oruvar kināṭi pārttumaruntukoṭuppavar(Physician), maṟṟoruvar kaṭṭi mutaliya vaṟṟi aṟuttuppuṇkaḷi āṟavippavar. avar calliyak kiriyi paṇṇuvāṉ (Surgeon) eṉappaṭṭār. ovvoru maruttuvamaṉiyilum vērkaḷiyum paccili kaḷiyum keṇṭuvaruvōr, avaṟṟi maruntākkuvōr, nōyāḷikaḷik kavaṉikkum tahtimār eṉpavar iruntaṉar eṉṟu kalveṭṭukkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
cōḻar kālap pērilakkiyaṅkaḷāṉa cintāmaṇi, kampa rāmāyaṇam mutaliya nūlkaḷilum maruntukaḷippaṟṟiyum maruttuvarkaḷip paṟṟiyum cila vivaraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vērkaḷiyum, paccilikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttum muṟi tamiḻarkkē uriya citta maruttuva muṟiyākum.
piṟkālattil nāvitarum aṟuvi vēliyil īṭupaṭ ṭiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ maruttuvar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. piḷḷip pēṟṟut tuṟiyil nāvitar maṉivimār īṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar. nāvitaṉ maṉivi maruttuvacci eṉappaṭṭāḷ.
ki.pi. 13 allatu 14- ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar eṉṟu karutap paṭupavar parimēlaḻakar.  ivar tirukkuṟaḷ, paripāṭal ivaṟṟukku uriyeḻutiyavar. ivar tirukkuṟaḷukku eḻutiyavuri ivaratu pala kali aṟivi naṉku veḷippaṭuttu kiṟatu. ipperiyār tirukkuṟaḷil uḷḷa maruntu eṉum atikārattiṟkut tiṟampaṭa uri vakuttuḷḷār. ivar kālattil maruttuvak kali nāṭṭil naṉṉiliyil iruntati nām itiliruntu naṉkaṟiyalām. ivar kūṟiyuḷḷa ceytikaḷik kāṇka:
paḻiya viṉiyālum kāraṇaṅkaḷālum makkaṭku vātam mutaliya piṇikaḷ varum. avaṟṟuḷ paḻaviṉiyāl varuvaṉa, atu muṭiyumpōtu allatu tīrā. ātalāl paḻaviṉi nōykaḷi oḻittu, piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷāl varum nōykaḷi oḻikkum maruntiṉ tiṟam kūṟukiṉṟār. uṇavu,ceyalkaḷ ovvāmiyāl piṇikaḷ tōṉṟum...
vātappakuti, pittappakuti, aippakuti eṉpaṉa mūṉṟu. uṇavu cuviyālum, vīriyaṅkaḷālum, aḷavālum porunta vēṇṭum. maṉam, moḻi, meykaḷāl ceyyum toḻilkaḷi avi varuntuvataṟku muṉṉē niṟuttiviṭavēṇṭum. ivi iraṇṭum aḷavōṭu illāmal mikutal, kuṟital ceyyiṉ, avi tattam niliyil nillāvāy varuntum. ivaṟṟāl, uṭampiṟku iyalpākiya nōy mūvakittu eṉpatum, avi tuṉpam ceyvataṟkuk kāraṇam iruvakittu eṉpatum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
uṇavu cerittatukkuriya kuṟikaḷāvaṉa yākki noymmi, karaṇaṅkaḷ toḻiṟku uriyavātal, paci mikutal mutaliyaṉa......
uṇṭatu cerittālum ataṉ payaṉākiya iratam aṟātu ātalāl atuvum aṟalvēṇṭum eṉpār, mikappacittu eṉṟār. māṟu koḷḷāmiyāvatu, uṇpavaṉ uṇavu aḷavōṭu māṟu koḷḷāmalum, kāla iyalpōṭu māṟukoḷḷāmalum, cuvi vīriyaṅkaḷāl tammuḷ māṟukoḷḷāmiyumām. avaṟṟik kuṟikkoḷḷātu maṉam pōṉapaṭi uṇṇiṉ, ataṉāl nōyum iṟappum uṇṭākum.
iṉpamāvatu, vātam mutaliya mūṉṟum tattam niliyil tiriyāmalum maṉam, moḻi, mey eṉpavi ataṉ vayattavātalum ataṉāl aṟam mutaliya nāṉkum eytalumām.


ayalnāṭṭār maruttuva muṟikaḷ
mokalāyarkaḷ celvākkup paraviya kālattil avarkaḷukkē uriya yūṉāṉi maruttuvam in nāṭṭil paraviyatu. ellā maruntukaḷilum iṉippu iruppatē yūṉāṉi maruttuvattiṉ ciṟappiyalpu. airōppiyar in nāṭṭil kuṭipukuntatu mutal mēṉāṭṭu maruttuva (allapati) muṟi in nāṭṭil paravattoṭaṅkiyatu. āṅkilēyar āṭciyil im muṟiyip parappavē maruttuvak kallūrikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟiya nākarikattil im muṟiyē peritum paravi viṭṭatu.
hōmiyōpati eṉṟa cermaṉ maruttuvamuṟiyum nāṭṭil ciṟitaḷavu paraviyuḷḷatu. maruttuvar cilar ip pala vaki maruttuva muṟikaḷiyum aṟintu, nōykku ēṟṟavāṟu ovvoru muṟiyik kiyāḷukiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam kiyāḷuvatē iṉṟu ciṟappākak karutappaṭukiṟatu.
mātar maruttuvam
ciṟṟūrk kiḻavikaḷ tēḷkaṭikku oru kuṟippiṭṭa pacciliyik kacakki ataṉ cāṟṟiṉi nukarac ceykiṉṟaṉar. koṭṭuvāyil veṅkāyatti vittut tēykkiṉṟaṉar; ciṟitunērattil vali maṟikiṟatu. ivvāṟu ap pāṭṭimār kuḻanti maruttuva muṟiyil ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟi nōkkap paṇṭik kālam mutalē tamiḻakattil ovvoru peṇmaṇiyum kuṭumpa maruttuva aṟivip peṟṟiruntāḷ eṉpatu veḷḷiṭi mali. āyiṉum navanākarikam mikunta ik kālattil nam peṇkaḷ kuṭumpa maruttuva aṟivil kuṟintuviṭṭaṉar eṉpatu varuntaṟkuriyatu.
citta maruttuvam
namatu tamiḻ maruttuvam citta maruttuvam eṉappaṭum. cittarkaḷ malik kukikaḷil iruntu tavam ceytavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ uṭali vaḷamāka vittukkoḷḷavum, nīṇṭa kālam vāḻavum pala maruntukaḷic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉar; malikaḷil iyaṟkiyākap payirākum palavaki maruntuc ceṭikaḷic cērttu ovvoṉṟaṉ kuṇattiyum kaṇṭaṟintaṉar; avaṟṟiṉ paccilikaḷ, pacci vērkaḷ, marap paṭṭikaḷ ivaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; kāynta vērkaḷ, ilikaḷ, paṭṭikaḷ ivaṟṟip pakkuvamākap poṭiyākkip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; malittēṉiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ap perumakkaḷ muṟinta elumpukaḷip poruttiṉar; tīrāta nōykaḷit tīrttaṉar; tam maruttuva muṟikaḷip pala pākkaḷākap pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. avi yāvum pala maruntu nūlkaḷāka veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ vittiya- vāta- yōka- ñāṉa cāttirat tiraṭṭu eṉpatu (12 pakutikaḷ) kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
nam tamiḻakattil potiya mali, kuṟṟāla mali, maruntu mali, tirukkaḻuk kuṉṟam mutaliya malippakutikaḷ maruntuc ceṭikaṭkup peyar peṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷākum.
tañci- āpirakām paṇṭitar aimpatu āṇṭukaṭku muṉ mikacciṟanta maruttuvarāy viḷaṅkiṉār; pala maruntuk kaṭikaḷi nāṭeṅkum tōṟṟuvittār. avaratu maruntakam tañciyil periya aḷavil paṇiyāṟṟiyatu. avar maṟiviṟkup piṉpu avar makkaḷ at tuṟiyin paṇiyāṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. kuṭumpa maruttuvattiṟku eṟṟa maruntukaḷ avarkaḷiṭam kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa; naṟpayaṉi aḷittu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu nāṭṭil ciṟanta citta maruttuvar āṅkāṅku irukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷi ūkkuvittal tamiḻar kaṭaṉākum.
muṭivuri
āyurvētam, yūṉāṉi, citta maruttuvam ākiya intiya nāṭṭu maruttuva muṟikaḷi āṅkila maruttuva muṟiyōṭu kaṟpikkac ceṉṉiyil oru kallūri irukkiṉṟatu. atil paṭittup palar paṭṭam peṟṟu vantu maruttuvat toḻil purintu varukiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum citta maruttuvattiṟkeṉṟu oru taṉik kallūri iruntāṟṟāṉ tamiḻ maruttuva muṟi muḻu vaḷarcciyi aṭitalkūṭum.
āṅkila maruttuva muṟi nāṭṭiṟ kālkoṇṭa piṟaku, nāṭṭu maruttuvam nammavarāl kiviṭappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. ataṉāl paṇpaṭṭa maruttuvar tōṉṟi nāṭṭu maruttuvatti vaḷarkka vaḻiyillāta nilimi uṇṭākiyuḷḷatu. tamiḻ- tamiḻar- tamiḻk kalikaḷ paṟṟiya maṟumalarcci tōṉṟivarum ik kālattil, maṟiyum niliyil uḷḷa tamiḻ maruttuvam ākiya citta maruttuvak kali yi vaḷarkka muṟpaṭutal tamiḻ makkaḷatu taliyāya kaṭaṉākum.

10. camayak kali
camaya uṇarcci
tamiḻar piṟamoḻi makkaḷuṭaṉ kalappiṉṟi vāḻnta kālattil, iṟantavar vaḻipāṭṭiyum, pāmpu vaṇakkattiyum, iliṅka vaṇakkattiyum koṇṭiruntaṉar. kuṟiñcinila makkaḷ murukaṉiyum, mulli nilattār māyōṉiyum, marutanila makkaḷ vēntaṉiyum (intiraṉiyum), neytal nilattār kaṭaliyum, pālinila makkaḷ kāḷiyiyum vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. paṭippaṭiyāka aṟivu vaḷara vaḷara, it teyvaṅkaṭkellām mēlāṉa paramporuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉṟa nampikki paḻan tamiḻarkku ēṟpaṭṭatu. ap paramporuḷ eṅkum taṅkiyiruk kiṉṟatu eṉṟa poruḷil ataṉi iṟi eṉṟaḻittaṉar; aḵtu ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭantuniṟpatu eṉṉum poruḷil ataṉik kaṭavuḷ eṉṟaṉar. ivi iraṇṭum muḻumutaṟ poruḷik kuṟikkum tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ. tiṇi nila makkaḷ avaravar aṟivu vaḷarccikkēṟpavum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaṭ kēṟpavum vaḻipāṭṭu muṟiyik kikkoṇṭaṉar; tattam nilaṅkaḷil kiṭikkum poruḷkaḷi vittut tattam teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.
caṅka kālattil
kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ muruka pūciyiṭṭu āṭippāṭiyatu kuṉṟak kuravi eṉappaṭṭatu. pālinila makkaḷ kāḷikkup pūciyiṭṭu āṭippāṭiyatu vēṭṭuva vari eṉappaṭṭatu. mulli nilattār kaṇṇaṉi nōkki āṭip pāṭiyatu āycciyar kuravi eṉappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟē maruta nilattār āṟukaḷil veḷḷam perukivarumpōtu āṟṟip pūcittu āṭippāṭiṉar; neytal nila makkaḷ cuṟāmīṉ mutukelumpi naṭṭuk kaṭal teyvatti vaḻipaṭṭup pōṟṟiṉar. ciṟṟūrkaḷil kirāma tēvatikaḷ varipāṭu naṭipeṟṟatu. pārāṭṭattakum paṇpuṭip periyōrkaḷ, pattiṉip peṇṭir mutaliyōrkkum kōvilkaḷ kaṭṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. itukāṟum kūṟivanta vaḻipāṭukaḷ aṉittum tamiḻarkkē uriya vaḻipāṭukaḷ.
camayak kalappu
vaṭa intiyāviliruntu vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tam vētaṅ kaḷuṭaṉum vētakāla vaḻipāṭukaḷuṭaṉum tamiḻakam pukuntaṉar. avarkaḷ tī, nīr, kāṟṟu mutaliya poruḷkaḷit teyvaṅ kaḷāka vaḻipaṭṭaṉar; maḻik kaṭavuḷākiya intiraṉi vaḻi paṭṭaṉar; aḻittaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa uruttiraṉi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar; tamiḻakattil nāḷaṭivil avartam celvākku mikutip paṭṭatu. tamiḻaracarkaḷ avarkaḷi vaḷam mikunta āṟṟōraṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟṟiṉar; avarkaṭku nilaṅkaḷiyum ūrkaḷiyum māṉiyamāka aḷittaṉar; avarkaḷik koṇṭu, atukāṟum tamiḻakattil illāta irāca cūya yākam, acuvamētam mutaliya yākaṅkaḷic ceyyat talippaṭṭaṉar. inta yākaṅkaḷil vētiyarē purōkitarāka iruntaṉar. piṉṉar nāḷaṭivil aracariṭam celvākkup peṟṟa kāraṇattāl vaṭamoḻip purōkitarkaḷum kurukkaḷum tamiḻakattuk kōvilkaḷil arccakarāka iṭam peṟṟaṉar. avarkaḷāl vaṭamoḻi tamiḻakak kōvilkaḷil pukuttappaṭṭatu. pūcikkuriya mantiraṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyilēyē collap peṟṟaṉa. ciṟukac ciṟukat tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kōvilkaḷil vaṭamoḻiyum vaṭavar vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷum kālappōkkil vērūṉṟiviṭṭaṉa. vaṭamoḻi tēvarkaḷukkup purinta moḻiyeṉṟum ataṉāl atu tēvamoḻi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭatu. vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta katikaḷ yāvum teṉṉintiyak kaṭavuḷarkku ēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa.
iliṅka mūrttamāka vaḻipaṭappeṟṟa civaṉ, vaṭavar vaḻipaṭṭu vanta uruttiraṉākak karutappaṭṭāṉ. vaṭavar koṇṭuvanta uruttiraṉ aḻittaṟ kaṭavuḷ; cuṭukāṭṭut teyvam; maṇṭiyōṭukaḷi māliyākak kōttut taliyilaṇintavaṉ; mayāṉattil naṭaṉamāṭupavaṉ; piṇac cāmpali pūcik koṇṭiruppavaṉ. uruttiraṉip paṟṟiya ik karuttukkaḷ civaṉukku ēṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. pālinilat tēvatiyāṉa koṟṟavi, turkki eṉṟu peyariṭap paṭṭu civaṉukku maṉiviyākkappaṭṭāḷ. murukaṉ kārttiki eṉṟa aṟuvarāl vaḷarkkappaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟa katiyōṭu toṭarpu paṭuttappaṭṭu aṟumukaṉ ākkappaṭṭāṉ; cuppiramaṇiyaṉ eṉṟa putiya peyariyum peṟṟāṉ; tēvēntiraṉ makaḷākiya teyvayāṉi avaṉukku maṉiviyākkappaṭṭāḷ.
ivvāṟu vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kūṭṭuṟavāl paḻantamiḻar camayattil camayak kalappu ēṟpaṭṭatu. vētaṅkaḷil uruttiraṉip paṟṟiyum piṟa teyvaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum eḻutap peṟṟirunta katikaḷellām tamiḻakattuc civa neṟiyilum māl neṟiyilum iṭam peṟṟaṉa. tēvarkaḷ acurarkaḷ katikaḷum, acurarkaḷi aḻikka iṟivaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa avatāraṅkaḷum piṟa muyaṟcikaḷum paṟṟiya katikaḷum, tamiḻakattiṟ paraviṉa. iṅṅaṉam paraviya ik katikaḷ caṅka nūlkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
vaṭanāṭṭuc camayaṅkaḷ
atē caṅka kālattil, vaṭanāṭṭil tōṉṟiya puttamum camaṇamum tamiḻakattil nuḻintaṉa. avi iraṇṭum maṉitari aṉpāṟ piṇikkum camayaṅkaḷ; cāti vēṟupāṭukaḷip pēcātavi; aṉpu vāḻkkiyē tēvi eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiyavi; āyiṉum kaṭumiyāṉa virataṅkaḷi yuṭiyavi; putta tuṟavikaḷum, camaṇat tuṟavikaḷum tāṅkaḷ vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷil irunta kūṭattil tamiḻ makkaḷukkuk kalvi kaṟpittaṉar; avarkaḷ vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷ paḷḷikaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ik kāraṇattālēyē kalvic cālikaḷ paḷḷikaḷ eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. camaṇarum puttarum potumakkaṭkuc camayak kalviyum potuk kalviyum kaṟpittaṉar; maruttuvam mutaliya utavikaḷip purintaṉar; oḻukka cīlarāy naṭantaṉar; ivaṟṟāl avarkaḷ celvākkut tamiḻakattil naṉku paraviyatu.
civa - viṇava neṟikaḷ
camaṇa putta nōṉpukaḷ piṉpaṟṟak kaṭumiyāṉavi. mēlum ulakap paṟṟē kūṭātu eṉpatu avar tam aḻuttamāṉa koḷki. tamiḻ makkaḷ ivaṟṟi muṟṟilum piṉpaṟṟa muṭiyavilli. ic camayattil vaṭa intiyāvil (ki.pi. 300-600) pakti neṟi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kōvilkaḷiluḷḷa kaṭavuḷar tiruvuruvaṅkaḷi naṉṟāka alaṅkarittal, avaṟṟiṟkup pūciyiṭutal, uṇavup poruḷkaḷiyellām vittup paṭittal, viḻākkaḷ ceytal, iṟivaṉi āṭiyum pāṭiyum tutittal- ivaṟṟik kūṟuvatē paktineṟi eṉpatākum. im muṟi tamiḻakattil paraviyatu. camaṇa puttarkaḷiṉ nōṉpukaḷilum piṟavaṟṟilum calippuṟṟa tamiḻ makkaḷ ip putiya neṟiyil pukuntaṉar. appoḻutu tamiḻ nāṭṭi āṇṭavar pallava, pāṇṭiyarāvar. avarkaḷ ip putiya neṟikku ākkamaḷittaṉar; perum poruḷ vaḻaṅkiṉar. maṉṉaratu ākkattālum makkaḷuṭiya viruppattālum ipputiya neṟi tamiḻakattil celvākkup peṟṟatu.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kūṭṭuṟavāl ip paktineṟi nuḻikkappaṭṭatu. ākavē, civaneṟi- civa neṟi eṉṟum, māl neṟi- viṇava neṟi eṉṟum vaṭa moḻi ilakkaṇappaṭi peyar māṟṟam peṟṟaṉa. ovvoru talattilum vaṭanāṭṭu muṉivar vantu tavam ceytatākavum, at talattip pūcittatākavum katikaḷ kaṭṭiviṭappaṭṭaṉa camayavātikaḷ ivaṟṟil mikka ārvattik kāṭṭiṉar. ovvoru kōvililum naṭaṉam, nāṭakam eṉpaṉa vaḷarcci peṟalāyiṉa. kōvil vēlikaḷ vaḷarat toṭaṅkiṉa. kōvilkaḷ uruvattil perukiṉa. potumakkaḷum aracarum kōvil tiruppaṇi purintaṉar. ovvōr ūrilum kōvil naṭunāyakamāka viḷaṅkat talippaṭṭatu. ivvāṟu kōvil uruvattilum celvattilum perukavē, kōvil āṭciyāḷar ēṟpaṭṭaṉar. ūrāṭci maṉṟattārum kōvilāṭciyiṟ paṅku koṇṭaṉar.
kōviṟ paṇiyāḷar
periya kōvilkaḷil palavakip paṇi makkaḷ vēli ceytaṉar. kōvil arccakar, ici vallunar, tēvāram ōtuvār, āṭumakaḷir eṉap pala vakiyiṉar iruntaṉar. paktip pāṭalkaḷukkēṟpa naṭikkum naṭikiyar ovvoru periya kōvililum iruntaṉar. avarkaḷil cilar pāṭṭilum vallava rāyiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ patiyilār, kaṇikiyar, taḷiyilār, māṇikkattār eṉap pala peyarkaḷ peṟṟiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ āṭiṉa naṭaṉa vakikaḷ iṉṟu nāmaṟiyumāṟu illi. civaṉ kōvilkaḷil tiruppatikam (tēvārap pāṭalkaḷ) viṇṇappam ceyyum toḻilil palar iruntaṉar. piṟkālac cōḻaṉ kālattil pala kōvilkaḷil nūl niliyaṅkaḷum, camaya nūlkaḷip pātukākkum tirukkikkōṭṭi maṇṭapaṅkaḷum, ilakkaṇam, camaya nūlkaḷ ivaṟṟik kaṟpikkum maṇṭapaṅ kaḷum, naṭaṉa cālikaḷum amintiruntaṉa. peruṅ kōvilkaḷil āṇṭiṉ ellā mātaṅkaḷilum viḻākkaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉa. viṇavar kōvilkaḷilum iṅṅaṉamē naṭipeṟṟaṉa.
maṭaṅkaḷ
cila periya kōvilkaḷi aṭuttu maṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟil camaya nūlaṟivilum oḻukkattilum ciṟanta tuṟavikaḷ iruntu,  potu makkaṭkuc camaya aṟivurikaḷ nikaḻtti vantaṉar. kōvilāṭciyil cila iṭaṅkaḷil maṭattut talivarkaḷukku iṭam tarappaṭṭatu.
cōḻa pāṇṭiyar āṭci vīḻnta piṟkālattil iṉṟuḷḷa maṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. matattip potu makkaṭku aṟivuṟutta matat tuṟavikaḷ tēvippaṭṭaṉar. piṟkālac ciṟṟaracarum celvarum potumakkaḷum am maṭaṅkaḷukku nilattiyum celvattiyum vāri vaḻaṅkiṉar. tiruvāṭutuṟi ātiṉam, tarumapuram ātīṉam, tiruppaṉantāḷ ātīṉam, tiruvaṇṇāmali ātīṉam eṉpaṉa cittānta civa maṭaṅkaḷ. viruttācalattu maṭam, mayilam maṭam, kuṭanti maṭam eṉpavi vīra civa maṭaṅkaḷ. akōpila maṭam, vāṉamāmali cīyar maṭam eṉpaṉa vētānta maṭaṅkaḷ. kuṭanti caṅkarāccāriyār maṭam viṇava maṭam. ivi toṭakkattil camayattoṇṭu ceytu vantaṉa; kālappōkkil nāṭṭiṉ āṭci māṟu pāṭṭālum camutāya māṟṟaṅkaḷālum, āṅkilak kalviyālum piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷālum tamakkuriya camayat toṇṭiṉic carivarac ceyya muṭiyavilli.
piṟkālattil
piṉ nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil karunāṭaka navāpukaḷiṉ āṭcik kālattil āṅkilēyar nāṭu piṭikkac ceyta pōrāṭṭaṅ kaḷiṉāl kōvilāṭci muṟi keṭṭatu. avvāṟē maṭaṅ kaḷiṉ varaṉmuṟic ceyalkaḷum keṭṭaṉa nāṭē kuḻappatti liruntatāl nāṭṭu makkaḷ itaṉik kavaṉikkavilli. āṅkila aracu ēṟpaṭṭatum, makkaḷ āṅkilam kaṟkat toṭaṅkiṉar. cilar kiṟittavarāyiṉar. kiṟittavat tuṟavikaḷiṉ camayattoṇṭu makkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷik koḷḷi koṇṭatu. attakiya toṇṭu (ilavacak kalvi, ilavaca maruttuvam, vāḻkkiyi uyarttal) piṟa camayaṅkaḷil illātati makkaḷ veṟuttaṉar. camaya nūlkaḷil niṟinta pulamiyum ciṟanta oḻukkamum illāta palar parampari urimi koṇṭāṭi kōvil arccakarākavum āṭciyāḷarākavum vantukoṇṭirunta muṟiyip potu makkaḷ veṟuttaṉar. makkaṭku nallaṟivu pukaṭṭa iyalāta niliyil viḻākkaḷ pala naṭipeṟalāyiṉa iṉṉa piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷāl, cōḻar kālac camaya naṭimuṟi ik kālattil kuṟintuviṭṭatu. iṉṟu peyaraḷavil kōviṟ ceyalkaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. kālattukkēṟṟavāṟu koḷḷattakkaṉa koṇṭu, taḷḷat takkaṉa taḷḷic camayatti vaḷarkka vēṇṭum eṉṟa aṟivu camutāyattil palarkku ēṟpaṭavilli.
camayattiṉ peyarāl camutāyam pala cātikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭatu. orē camutāyattic cārnta makkaḷuḷ pala nūṟu cātikaḷ uḷa. oruvarōṭu oruvar koḷvaṉi koṭuppaṉi illi; uṇavu uṇpatilli; neruṅkip paḻakuvatumilli. inta niliyil ivarkaḷi uṟuppiṉarkaḷākak koṇṭa camayam eppaṭi muḻuvaṉmiyuṭaṉ vāḻamuṭiyum? eṉavē, iṉṟu camayam eṉpatu peyaraḷavil iruntu varukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruntum.
putta camayam
kiṟittup perumāṉukku muṉṉarē in nāṭṭil putta camayat tuṟavikaḷ iruntaṉar. avarkaḷil palar malik kukikaḷil vāḻntaṉar; cilar maṭaṅkaḷiyum kōvilkaḷiyum kaṭṭi makkaṭku camayap piraccāram ceytaṉar. uyirkkoli nīkkutal, ellā uyirkaḷiṭattum aṉpāyiruttal, peṟṟōriyum periyōriyum matittu naṭantu koḷḷutal eṉpaṉa putta camayak koḷkikaḷ. ac camayat tuṟavikaḷ pikku (piṭcu) eṉṟum, tuṟavu pūṇṭa peṇmaṇi pikkuṇi (piṭcuṇi) eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉar. acōkaṉ kālattil puttam tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ paraviyatu; ilaṅkiyilum paraviyatu. putta vikāraṅkaḷ caṅka kālattil pala tamiḻ nakaraṅkaḷil iruntaṉa. iḷampōtiyār mutaliya oru cilar caṅka kālattil vāḻnta putta camayap pulavarāvar.
camaṇa camayam
puttttiṟku muṉṉarē tamiḻ nāṭṭil camaṇa camayam nuḻintirukkalām eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu. piṉpaṟṟutaṟku arumiyāṉa nōṉpukaḷik koṇṭatu camaṇa camayam. camaṇa muṉivarkaḷ āṭiyaṟṟavarkaḷ; talimuṭi vaḷarttavarkaḷ; kuḷiyātavarkaḷ; eṟumpukkum tuṉpam varalākātu eṉṟa karuttuṭaṉ mayiṟpīlik kaṟṟiyik koṇṭu vaḻiyit tūymi ceytu naṭappavar. it tikampara camaṇa muṉivar perumpālōr malik kukikaḷil vāḻntaṉar; oru cilar nakaraṅkaḷil paḷḷikaḷiyum pāḻikaḷiyum kaṭṭi vāḻntaṉar. paḷḷi eṉpatu maṭam; pāḻi eṉpatu kōvil. camaṇar arukatēvaṉi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. tāṉāka iṟanta uyirit tiṉṉalām eṉpatu puttar koḷki. ataṉiyum tiṉṉa lākātu eṉpatu camaṇar koḷki. muṟṟum tuṟantārkkē vīṭu; peṇkaḷ muṟṟum tuṟakka iyalātākiyāl avarkaḷ ip piṟappil camaṇar koḷkikaḷip piṉpaṟṟiṉāl aṭutta piṟaviyil āṭavarāyp piṟappar, piṉpu muṟṟum tuṟakkalām- tuṟantāl vīṭu peṟalām eṉpatu camaṇar koḷki.
muṟṟum tuṟanta periyōrkku vaḻaṅkuvatu tāṉam; amāvāci, purṇami, aṭṭami ākiya nāṭkaḷil uṇṇā nōṉpu mēṟkoḷvatē tavam; īkkaḷik koṉṟu tēṉeṭuttalāl tēṉi uṇṇalākātu; aṟivi mayakkum kaḷḷiyum parukalākātu; piṟa uyirkaḷik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉi uṇṇalākātu; iṅṅaṉam iruppatē cīlam (oḻukkam) eṉappaṭum. acōka marattiṉaṭiyil mūṉṟu kuṭikaḷiṉ kīḻ amarntuḷḷa aruka tēvaṉi malar, puki, cāntam, viḷakku ivaṟṟāl vaḻipaṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam tāṉam, tavam, cīlam, arukatēvaṉ pūcaṉi ākiya nāṉkum tavaṟātu ceypavar vīṭupēṟu aṭipavar eṉpatu camaṇak koḷki.
ik koḷkikaḷ tamiḻ makkaḷāl kaṭumiyākap piṉpaṟṟa muṭiyavilli. civarum, vitikarum, puttarum, viṇavarum camaṇa camayatti vaṉmiyāka etirttaṉar. avvāṟē vitikarum camaṇarum puttatti etirttaṉar. ki.pi. 7,8,9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil palamāṉa camayap pōrkaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟāl camaṇamum puttamum tam celvākki iḻantaṉa. ataṉāl vitikac civamum, vitika viṇamum vaḷarcciyuṟat toṭaṅkiṉa. āyiṉ, mikac cila iṭaṅkaḷil camaṇarum, puttarum iruntu camayat toṇṭukaḷiyum ilakkiyap paṇiyiyum iṭiviṭātu ceytaṉar eṉpatu kalveṭṭukkaḷālum piṟkāla nūlkaḷālum terikiṉṟatu.

11. tattuvak kali
(1) camaṇa camaya tattuvam
camaṇa camayam
camaṇar (ramaṇar) eṉpataṟkut tuṟavikaḷ eṉpatu poruḷ. tuṟavi vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟi, tuṟavu pūṇṭavarē vīṭupeṟuvar eṉpatu ic camayak koḷki. tuṟavu- camaṇam. eṉavē, itu camaṇa camayam eṉappaṭṭatu. pulaṉkaḷiyum viṉikaḷiyum jayittavar ic camaya ācāriyārkaḷ ātalāl jiṉar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. jiṉari vaḻipaṭum camayam jiṉa camayam eṉappaṭṭatu. ic camayak kaṭavuḷukku arukaṉ eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu. ātalāl arukaṉi vaḻipaṭum camayam ārukata camayam eṉappaṭṭatu; arukaṉi vaṇaṅkiṉōr ārukatar (Arhat) eṉappaṭṭaṉar.
camaṇa camayak koḷkikaḷi ulakattil parappap periyārkaḷ avvappōtu tōṉṟuvar eṉpatu camaṇa camayak koḷki. ituvari 24 periyavarkaḷ (tīrttaṅkarar) tōṉṟiṉar. avaruḷ mutalvar viruṣapa tēvar (ātipakavaṉ); 24-ām tīrttaṅkarar varttamāṉa makāvīrar eṉṟu ic camayattār kūṟuvar. āyiṉ, varalāṟṟu āciriyar, puttar kālattil vāḻntirunta varttamāṉa tīrttaṅkarari maṭṭum oppuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. avar kālam ki.mu. 6-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. avarukku makāvīrar eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu.
camaṇa camayam piṟkālattil tikampara camaṇam, cuvē tāmpara camaṇam, tāṉakavāci camaṇam eṉa mūṉṟākap pirintatu. tikamparar- ticikaḷi āṭiyāka uṭuttavar; āṭiyiṉṟi iruppavar. cuvātāmpara camaṇar- veṇṇiṟa āṭi aṇipavar. ivviruvarum uruva vaḻipāṭṭiṉar. tāṉakavāci camaṇar- camaṇa ākama nūlkaḷi maṭṭum vittu avaṟṟiyē tīrttaṅkararākavum, arukak kaṭavuḷākavum karuti vaṇaṅkuvar. tamiḻakattil tikampara camaṇarē iruntaṉar. tikampara camaṇam ki.mu. 3-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻakattil nuḻintatu. avarkaḷ āṭiyiṉṟi iruntatāl amaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.
camaṇa camaya tattuvam
camaṇa camaya tattuvattil oṉpatu poruḷkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi nava patārttam eṉappaṭum. ivi, uyir, uyirallatu, puṇṇiyam, pāpam, ūṟṟu, ceṟippu, utirppu, kaṭṭu, vīṭu eṉappaṭum.
(1) uyirkaḷ pala; aḻivaṟṟavi. kaṭavuḷ uyirkaḷip paṭikkavilli. uyirkaḷ ōraṟivuyir mutal aiyaṟivuyir īṟāka aintu vakippaṭum. aiyaṟivuyirkaḷ maṉam uṭiyavi, maṉam illātavi eṉa iruvakippaṭum. (maṉam- pakuttaṟivu). uyir nalviṉi tīviṉikaḷic ceytu avaṟṟiṉ payaṉākiya iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷit tuyppataṟkum narakakati, vilaṅkukati, makkaḷkati, tēvakatikaḷil piṟantu iṟantu uḻaṉṟu tiriyum; kālappōkkil iruviṉikaḷiyum aṟuttup pēriṉpa vīṭṭiṉi aṭiyum. iḵtu uyirkaḷiṉ iyalpu makkaḷākap piṟanta uyirkaḷ maṭṭumē vīṭupēṟu peṟum. makkaḷuḷḷum tuṟavikaḷē vīṭupēṟṟi aṭivar. illaṟattārkku vīṭu pēṟu illi.
(2) kālam, ākāyam mutaliyaṉa uyiraṟṟavi. iviyum toṉṟutoṭṭu iruppavi.
(3) (4) puṇṇiyam pāvam: nalla eṇṇam, nalla col, nalla ceyki ivaṟṟāl peṟappaṭṭa puṇṇiyam, uyirkaḷi maṉitarākavum, tēvarākavum piṟakkac ceyyum. tīya eṇṇam, tīya col, tīya ceyalkaḷ ivaṟṟāl uṇṭāṉa pāvam, uyirkaḷi naraka katiyilum ulaka katiyilum piṟakkac ceytu tuṉpuṟuttum.
(5) ūṟṟu: nalviṉi, tīviṉi iraṇṭum uyiril curappavi. ivi eṇṇam, col, ceyal ivaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyāka uyiriṭam cērkiṉṟaṉa.
(6) ceṟippu: iruviṉikaḷum curakkum ūṟṟiṉatu vaḻiyi aṭittu viṭuvatu ceṟippu eṉappaṭum. uyir oru piṟappil ceyta iruviṉikaḷi maṟupiṟappil tuyttu nīkkumpoḻutē putiya viṉikaḷ vantu cērātapaṭi taṭuttalē ceṟippu eṉappaṭum.
(7) iṅṅaṉam iruviṉiyiṉ ūṟṟu mēlum perukāmal taṭutta piṉpu, tuyttuk kaḻikkāmal eñci niṉṟa viṉikaḷit tavam mutaliyavaṟṟāl nīkki viṭutal utirppu eṉappaṭum.
(8) maṉam, moḻi, mey, aimpulaṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl uṇṭākum viṉikaḷ uyiruṭaṉ kalappatu kaṭṭu eṉappaṭum.
(9) aimpulaṉkaḷi avittu viṉikaḷiliruntu nīṅkiya uyir kaṭiyilā aṟivu- kāṭci- vīriyam- iṉpam ivaṟṟi aṭintu vīṭupēṟu peṟutal vīṭu eṉappaṭum.
vīṭu peṟṟa uyirē kaṭavuḷ eṉpatu camaṇa camayak karuttu. mēlē kūṟappeṟṟa oṉpatu poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇmiyi aṟivatu naṉñāṉam eṉappaṭum; avaṟṟiṉ taṉmiyi uṇarvatu naṟkāṭci eṉappaṭum. ivviraṇṭiyum uḷattiṟ koṇṭu oḻukuvatē nalloḻukkam eṉappaṭum. im mūṉṟum irattiṉat tirayam eṉṟum mummaṇi eṉṟum collap paṭum. ivi mūṉṟum vīṭu pēṟṟukku iṉṟiyamiyātavi. (mayili cīṉi vēṅkaṭacāmi, camaṇamum tamiḻum, pak. 1-5)
(2) putta camaya tattuvam
putta camayam
puttar aracakumārar; ki.mu. 6-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar; uyirkaḷiṉ tuṉpatti oḻikkat tuṟavu pūṇṭu muṉivar palaruṭaṉ ceṉṟu, tattuva vicāraṇi ceytu, iṟutiyil pōti (araca) mara niḻalil amarntu yōkattil iruntār. appōtu avarukku viḷaṅkiya uṇmikaḷē putta camaya tattuvam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. avar aṉṟu mutal puttar (ñāṉi) eṉṟaḻikkappaṭṭār. putta camayam puttar kālattil oṉṟākavē iruntatu. piṉpu, atu iraṇṭākap pirintatu. puttar kālac camayam hīṉayāṉam eṉappaṭṭatu; piṉpu tōṉṟiyatu makāyāṉam eṉappaṭṭatu.
puttar karuttukkaḷ
ulaka vāḻvu tuṉpakkaṭal- at tuṉpattit toṭarcciyāka vaḷarppatu maṟupiṟappu- makkaḷ aṭiyattakum mēlāṉa uṟutipporuḷ piṟavāmiyē - paṟṟaṟṟup payaṉ nōkkātu ceyyum naṟceyalkaḷē ap piṟavāmikkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷākum; eṉpaṉa puttar kaṇṭaṟinta uṇmikaḷ cuyanalattik karuti muyalvatu aṟamaṉṟu-am muyaṟci ulakappaṟṟiṉi vaḷarttukkoṇṭē varuvatu. ātalāl payaṉ karutāmal piṟar poruṭṭuc ceyyum ceyalē, tuṉpantarum ulakap paṟṟiṉi aṟuppatu eṉpatu puttar koḷki.
puttar uritta aṟam
(1) nāṉku niṉivukaḷ: (1) uṭal tūymiyaṟṟatu eṉṟu niṉittal, (2) nukarvukaḷiliruntu uṇṭākum kēṭukaḷi niṉittal, (3) eṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ niliyaṟṟa taṉmiyi niṉittal, (4) iruppukku iyalpāṉa taṉmikaḷi niṉittal.
(2) nāṉku naṉmuyaṟcikaḷ: (1) tīya kuṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭākāmal taṭukka muyalutal, (2) piṉṉarē uṇṭāki yirukkum tīyakuṇaṅkaḷi vilakka muyalutal, (3) muṉpu illāta naṉmiyi uṇṭākka muyalutal, (4) muṉpu uṇṭākiyuḷḷa naṉmiyi mikippaṭutta muyalutal.
3. cittiyip peṟutaṟkuriya nāṉku vaḻikaḷ: (1) iruttiyip peṟavēṇṭum eṉṉum niṉippu, (2) iruttiyip peṟuvataṟkuriya muyaṟci, (3) iruttiyip peṟuvataṟkuriya maṉanili, (4) iruttiyip peṟuvataṟkuriya ārāycci.
4. aivaki āṟṟalkaḷ: (1) srītti, (2) tiṟami, (3) niṉivu, (4) uruvēṟṟal, (5) ūkittal.
5. uṇmi ñāṉatti uṇṭākkuvataṟkuriya karuvikaḷ: (1) tiṟami, (2) niṉivu, (3) uruvēṟṟal(maṉaṉam), (4) tiripiṭaka ārāycci. (cūttirappiṭakam, viṉayapiṭakam, apitarma piṭakam eṉṉum mūvakippaṭṭa putta ākamat tokuti)  (5) makiḻcci, (6) amiti, (7) otta pārvi (camatiruṣṭi).
ivaṟṟiṉ vaḻiniṉṟu paṟṟi aṟukka vēṇṭum. paṟṟi aṟuppataṟku vēṟu iruvaki vaḻikaḷum uṇṭu. avi aintu pāvaṉikaḷ, nāṉku tiyāṉaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa.
1. aintu pāvaṉikaḷ: (1) mittirī pāvaṉi, (2) karuṇā pāvaṉi, (3) mutitapāvaṉi, (4) acupapāvaṉi; (5) upēṭcā pāvaṉi.
1. mittirīpāvaṉi: putta tuṟavi,ellā uyirkaḷum pērāci; nōy, tuṉpam ivaṟṟiliruntu viṭupaṭṭuk kaḷippuṟṟu vāḻaṭṭum eṉṟu pāvittal.
2. karuṇāpāvaṉi: vaṟiyavar vaṟumi nīṅkic celvam peṟuka eṉṟu pāvittal.
3. mutita pāvaṉi: ovvoruvarum tattamakku amintuḷḷa nalviṉippayaṉi aṭivārāka eṉṟu pāvittal. (mutitam- makiḻcci)
4. acupapāvaṉi: uṭal mikavum iḻintatu- veṟuttaṟ kuriya nāṟṟatti veḷippaṭuttuvatu eṉṟu pāvittal. (acupam- amaṅkalam)
5. upēṭcāpāvaṉi: ellā uyirkaḷiyum oṉṟākak karututalum, viruppu veṟuppiṉṟi iruttalum ākum. (upēṭci- utācīṉam, alaṭciyam.)
nāṉku tiyāṉaṅkaḷ
mutalām tiyāṉam: tuṟavi, kāmam pāvam ivaṟṟiliruntu taṉṉi vilakki maṉaṉattōṭum ārāycciyōṭum kūṭiyirukkumpōtu anupavikkum makiḻcci nili.
iraṇṭām tiyāṉam: maṉaṉamum ārāycciyum illāmal avi aṭikkappaṭṭu irukkumpōtu, amitiyiliruntu uṇṭākum iṉpanili.
mūṉṟām tiyāṉam: tuṟavi, kāmam mutaliyavaṟṟi oḻittu, makiḻcciyip peṟṟu, poṟumi aṟivu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu kūṭiya iṉpattit taṉṉuḷ anupavittal.
nāṉkām tiyāṉam: iṉpamum illāmal tuṉpamum illāmal irukkiṉṟa otta pārviyum aṟiviyum koṇṭuḷḷa tūya nili.
it tiyāṉaṅkaḷ nāṉkum camātikku utaviyāyuḷḷaṉa.
putta camayattiṉarkkup puttar, tarmam, caṅkam eṉṉum mūṉṟum ciṟantavi. ivi, mummaṇikaḷ eṉappaṭum. mēlē collappeṟṟavi putta tarmattic cērntavi. ivi allāmal putta tarmattil, tāy tantiyar- makkaḷukkuriya oḻukkaṅkaḷ, āciriyar- māṇavarkkuriya oḻukkaṅkaḷ, naṇpar oruvariṭattil oruvar naṭantukoḷḷattakum oḻukkaṅkaḷ, mutalāḷikkum toḻilāḷikkum uriya oḻukkaṅkaḷ, illaṟattārkkum tuṟavikkum uriya oḻukkaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa virivākavum teḷivākavum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tuṟavikaḷ, mēlē collappeṟṟa cittikkuriya tuṟikaḷil veṟṟi peṟṟāl, parinirvāṇam eṉṉum tuṉpamaṟṟa niliyi aṭivar eṉṟu putta nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa! (maṇimēkali, pak.96-107)
3. atvitam
caṅkarar
ivar, ki.pi. 8-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil maliyāḷa māvaṭṭatti luḷḷa kālaṭi eṉṉum ūril piṟantavar. ivar civa camayattic cērntavar. ivaratu koḷki atvitam eṉappaṭum.
ulakattil kāṇappaṭum poruḷkaḷ yāvum nam pulaṉkaḷiṉuṭiya maruṭciyiṉāl kāṇappaṭum tōṟṟaṅkaḷē allāmal uṇmiyāṉavi alla.
nam kaṇkaḷukkut terintuḷḷa ulakattilum teriyāta ulakaṅkaḷilum paravi, uyirkkuyirāka irukkiṉṟa oṉṟē piramam eṉpatu. collaḷavu appiramam vēṟu, ulakam vēṟu eṉṟu collalām. āṉāl, ulakam piramatti ātāramākak koṇṭu ataṉkaṇṇē irukkiṉṟatu. ātalāl, ulakam, piramam ākiya iraṇṭum vēṟu alla- iraṇṭu alla- iraṇṭum aṟṟa nili. inta iraṇṭum aṟṟa niliyē vaṭa moḻiyil atvitam eṉappaṭum.
piramam ulakamākap pariṇamippatum, ulakam piramattuḷ oṭuṅkuvatum inta atvita cittāntattiṉ viti. cātāraṇa aṟivilpiramam eṉavum, ulakam eṉavum iraṇṭākat tōṉṟiṉālum, ñāṉa neṟiyil ellām piramamākavē kāṇappaṭuvatāl, ivi parañāṉam, apara ñāṉattiṟkuriya uṭkaruviyākiya maṉattiṉkaṇ aruḷuṇarvāyt tōṉṟu kiṟatu. centāmari cemmi + tāmari. paṇpum poruḷum pōla iraṇṭāyum, paṇpippōla oṉṟāyum irukkum taṉmiyē atvitam.
atvitak koḷkiyiṉpaṭi, uyir uṭaliṉum vēṟupaṭṭatu; uṇmi yāṉatu; ulakap paṟṟukkaḷiliruntu viṭupaṭṭavuṭaṉ iṟivaṉiṭam cērvatu. maṉitaṉ aṟivu iruvakippaṭum. avi, (1) aparañāṉam, (2) parañāṉam eṉpaṉa.
(1) kaṇ, kātu pōṉṟa poṟi, pulaṉkaḷālum, maṉam, patti, cittam, akaṅkāram mutaliya antakkaraṇaṅkaḷālum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟa inta ulakam paṟṟiya aṟivē cātāraṇa aṟivu *(aparañāṉam). inta aṟivu, meypōlat tōṉṟip poyyāki niliyaṟṟu oḻivatu.
(2) inta ulakiṟku ātāramāy irukkiṉṟa piramattippaṟṟiya aṟivē mēlāṉa aṟivu (parañāṉam) anta aṟivu niliyāṉatu. piramatti aṟiyum vaḻi pāramārttikam eṉappaṭum. uyir mēlāṉa aṟivi aṟintatum, ataṉip paṟṟiyirunta paṟṟukkaḷ akaṉṟu, anta niliyil atu ātmā eṉap peyar peṟum. parañāṉattip peṟṟa anta ātmā iṟivaṉuṭaṉ kalantu viṭukiṟatu.
māyi
ulakattiṉ niliyāmiyē māyi eṉpatu. iṭam, kālam, kāraṇam mutaliyavi nam anupavattiṉ ātāramāy iruntum, meypōl tōṉṟip poyyāy maṟikiṉṟaṉa. ivi, tammuḷ muraṇāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvulaka aṟivu ek kālattilum poruttamaṟṟa aṟivāka irukkiṉṟatu. namatu uḷḷuṇarvu mikumpōtu ivvulakam nam niṉiviliruntu maṟikiṉṟatu. cātāraṇa aṟivu (aparañāṉam) koṇṭu nām kāṇum poruḷkaḷ māyi eṉappaṭum. im maṟiyum ulakattiyum, ataṉ poruḷkaḷiyum māyi eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟuvar. ulakattiṟku uṇmittaṉmi illi eṉṟu maṉatti maṟippatum māyit taṉmitāṉ.
niliyaṟṟa ivvulakattiyum (ulaka nikaḻccikaḷ) niliyuḷḷa ulakattiyum (piramattōṭu kūṭiya ulakattiyum) orē camayattil nām anupavikka muṭiyātu. in niliyaṟṟa ulakamum niliyāṉa ulakattāl nilipeṟukiṉṟatu. āṉāl nam ciṟiya anupavamum, ivvuṇmiyik kāṭṭuvatilli. uḷḷuṇarvāltāṉ iti uṇara muṭiyum.
māyi eṉṟa iruḷāl, uyir, taṉ mēlāṉa aṟivu kuṉṟikkiṭakkiṟatu. mēlāṉa aṟivē inta iruḷi akaṟṟa muṭiyum. piramattip paramāṉmā eṉṟum, uyiri jīvāṉmā eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iraṇṭum oṉṟē āyiṉum uṭampu māyi ivaṟṟāl kaṭṭuppaṭṭa jīvāṉmāvi nōkkap paramāṉmā vēṟupaṭṭatāka aṟiñar upacarittuk kūṟuvar.
iṟivaṉi aṭiyum vaḻikaḷ
uyir iṟivaṉi aṭiya mūṉṟu nilikaḷik kaṭattal vēṇṭum. avi, (1) kuruviṭam payiṟci peṟutal, (2) niṉittup pārttal (Reflection), (3) vaḻipāṭu eṉpaṉa.
(1) mutalil kuruviṭam payiṟci peṟutal vēṇṭum; taṉakkum ulakap poruḷkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu eṉṉa eṉpatu pōṉṟa uṇmikaḷik kuruviṉ vāyilāka aṟitalē mutal nili ākum.
(2) iṅṅaṉam kuruviṉiṭam aṟinta uṇmikaḷit taṉ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil naṭipeṟum nikaḻccikaḷik koṇṭu aṟitalē iraṇṭām nili.
(3) kuruviṉ vāyilāka iṟivaṉip paṟṟit tāṉ kēṭṭa uṇmikaḷit taṉ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil anupavitta maṉitaṉ, taṉatu tiyāṉattiṉ vāyilākat teḷivāka aṟikiṉ ṟāṉ. inta niliyil avaṉ piramatti uṇaruvatōṭu, tāṉē piramamākavum ākiṉṟāṉ. inta niliyi aṭipavaṉē cīvaṉ muktaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ivaṉi ellāp paṟṟukkaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭṭa maṉitaṉ eṉṟum kūṟalām. ivaṉatu ātmā uṭaliviṭṭup pirintatum iṟivaṉōṭu kalantu viṭukiṉṟatu.
maṉita vāḻkki nīrōṭṭamuḷḷa āṟṟunīrk kumiḻiyi okkum; kumiḻiyum nīrtāṉ; ōṭum āṟṟilum atē nīrtāṉ irukkiṟatu. atē pōṉṟu cīvātmāvum paramātmāvum oṉṟē. ituvē atvita tattuvattiṉ uyirnāṭi. inta atvita tattuvam ēkāṉma vātam eṉṟum collappaṭum.
(4) viciṭṭāttuvitam
itu irāmānucar upatēcitta camaya tattuvam. ivar ki.pi. 11- ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar; mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattiṉar.
āṉmāviṟkum uṭampiṟkum uḷḷa toṭarpu iraṇṭaṟṟa (atvita) campantam. atupōlavē āṉmāvukkum iṟivaṉukkum uḷḷa iraṇṭaṟṟa toṭarpum attuvita toṭarpē. āṉāl āṉmāviṟkum iṟivaṉukkum uḷḷa iraṇṭaṟṟa toṭarpu, āṉmāviṟkum uṭampiṟkum uḷḷa iraṇṭaṟṟa muṟiyiviṭa viciṭṭamāṉatu (ciṟantatu) paṟṟi viciṭṭāttuvitam eṉappaṭṭatu. cīvāṉmā urimiyaṟṟatu; paramāṉmā urimiyuṭiyatu; (vicēṭamāṉatu), itu paṟṟiya attuvitam viciṭṭāttuvitam eṉappaṭṭatu.
(1) ulakam, piramam ivi vevvēṟu āyiṉum oṉṟu eṉpatu atvitam. ulakam, piramam ellām piramattiṉ uruvē. eṉavē, māyi eṉa oṉṟu illi eṉpatu viciṭṭāttuvitam.
(2) uyir piramattiṉ ellā amcaṅkaḷiyum uṭiyatu eṉpatu attuvitam..  paṭippu, ilakkumi nāyakattaṉmi eṉṟa iraṇṭum nīṅkalāka piramattiṟku uriya ellām uyirukkum uṇṭu eṉpatu viciṭṭāttuvitam.
(3) mutti eṉpatu pirama ñāṉattāl maṭṭumē uṇṭāvatu eṉpatu attuvitam. muttikkup paktiyē aṭippaṭi; ñāṉa paktiyē ciṟappāṉatu eṉpatu viciṭṭāttuvitam.
(4) piramamum uyirum oṉṟē eṉpatu attuvitam; piramam- uyir uṟavu, āṇṭāṉ- aṭimi uṟavu pōṉṟatu eṉpatu viciṭṭāttuvitam.
(5) piramamum uyirum oṉṟē eṉal attuvitam; piramamum uyirum nāyaka- nāyaki uṟavu koṇṭavi eṉpatu viciṭṭāttuvitam.
(6) tattuvamaci- nītāṉ atu.
	akam prammāmi - nāṉē piramam.
ivi attuvitak karuttukkaḷ.
		ōm namō nārāyaṇāya- eṭṭeḻuttu
		srīmat nārāyaṇa caraṇu caraṇam pirapatyē
		srīmat nārāyaṇāya nama:
ivi viciṭṭāttuvita mantiraṅkaḷ.
uyir vīṭupēṟṟi aṭiya iraṇṭu vaḻikaḷ uṇṭu. avi pirāpti eṉṟum pakti eṉṟum collappaṭum. pirāpti eṉpatu viṇavattil koṇṭa nampikki vaḻiyāka vīṭupēṟu aṭital; upaniṭataṅkaḷil kāṭṭappaṭum pakti neṟippaṭi vīṭaṭiya mūṉṟu paṭikaḷik kaṭattal vēṇṭum. avi karmayōkam, ñāṉayōkam, pattiyōkam eṉappaṭum.
(1) oruvaṉ taṉ kaṭamikaḷic ceytalum, taṉṉiyē kaṭavuḷukku urimiyākkutal evvāṟu eṉpati uṇartalum karmayōkam eṉappaṭum. (2) karmayōkattil paḻakiyavaṉ taṉṉuṭiya uṇmiyāṉa niliyiyum, niliyāmiyākiya taṉmiyiyum, taṉakkum kaṭavuḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpiyum uṇarvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam uṇarum muyaṟci ñāṉayōkam eṉappaṭum. (3) paktiyōkam eṉpatu kuruṭṭu nampikkiyaṉṟu; uyarnta aṟiviṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa kaṭavuḷip paṟṟiya tiyāṉattil uṇmiyāṉa nampikkiyum aṉpum koḷvatē paktiyōkam. im mūṉṟu nilikaḷiyum kaṭanta uyirē vīṭupēṟṟi aṭiyum. ulakamum uyirum kaṭavuḷāl ākkappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē ulakam māyi aṉṟu. ulaka niliyāmi māttiram koṇṭu ulakam illi eṉak kūṟutal uṇmiyaṉṟu.
5. tuvitam
itaṉik kaṇṭaṟintavar mātvāccāriyār. ivar teṉkaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil uṭuppi eṉṉum ciṟṟūril piṟantavar. ivar 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar (1199- 1278). ivar irāmānucarip pōlavē taṉippaṭṭa kaṭavuḷiṭam nampikki koṇṭavar. kaṭavuḷ vētaṅkaḷ tuṇiyālum aṟiya muṭiyātavar. avar muṭivillāta aruḷiyum āṟṟaliyum koṇṭavar. taṉippaṭṭa uyir, ulakam, nēram ellām kaṭavuḷ aruḷāl iruppavi; avar aruḷ illāviṭil aḻipavi. ivi yāvum kaṭavuḷukkākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉmūlam avar aṭivatu oṉṟumilli eṉpatu ivar karuttu.
ivar piramattil pala poruḷkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpar; iṟivaṉum uyirum, iṟivaṉum ulakamum, uyirum ulakamum, uyirum maṟṟōr uyirum, iṟivaṉ taṉittum maṟṟavikaḷuṭaṉ kalantum vāḻalām. uyirkaḷum ulakamum taṉittu iyaṅkum taṉmiyaṟṟaṉa. iṟivaṉ uyiriyum poruḷkaḷiyum āṇṭu varupavaṉē tavirap paṭikkavō, aḻikkavō illi. iṟivaṉ ivaṟṟiṟkut tuṇiyākavē viḷaṅkukiṟāṉ. iṟivaṉ eṅkum parantuḷḷāṉ. eṉavē iṟivaṉōṭu uyirkaḷ cārntu iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. aṇuvip pōṉṟa uyirum, taṉatu aṟivut taṉmiyāl ulakam muḻuvatum parantiruk kiṉṟatu. iraṇṭu uyirkaḷ orē taṉmiyuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil ovvoṉṟiṉ anupavamum vēṟu paṭukiṉṟatu.
6. civa cittāntam
cittānta cāttiraṅkaḷ
ki.pi. 400- 600 eṉṉum kāla elliyil ceyyappaṭṭatu tirumantiram. ataṉkaṇ mūvāyiram pāṭalkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil civa cittāntak karuttukkaḷ citaṟik kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. piṉ vanta appar, campantar mutaliyōr pāṭalkaḷilum civa cittāntak karuttukkaḷ citaṟik kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ik karuttukkaḷ aṉittiyum muṟippaṭuttik kūṟukiṉṟa civa cittānta cāttiraṅkaḷ patiṉāṉku ākum. avi ki.pi. 12, 13, 14- ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil ceyyappaṭṭavi. avi (1) tiruvuntiyār, (2) tirukkaḷiṟṟup paṭiyār, (3) civañāṉa pōtam, (4) civañāṉa cittiyār, (5) irupā- irupaḵtu, (6) uṇmi viḷakkam, (7) civappirakācam, (8) tiruvaruṭ payaṉ, (9) viṉā veṇpā, (10) pōṟṟip paḵṟoṭi, (11) koṭikkavi, (12) neñcu viṭutūtu, (13) uṇmineṟi viḷakkam, (14) caṅkaṟpa nirākaraṇam eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ talimaṇi, meykaṇṭār iyaṟṟiya civañāṉa pōtam. ip patiṉāṉku cāttiraṅkaḷiṉ cattupporuḷkaḷē civa camaya cattuvam eṉpatu.
kaṭavuḷ uyirkaḷuṭaṉ attuvitamāka niṉṟu avikaṭku viṉip payaṉi ūṭṭuvār. uyirkaṭkup pācattoṭarpāl piṟappu iṟappu nikaḻum. uyir paruvuṭal, aṟikaruvikaḷ, nuṇṇuṭal, pirāṇavāyu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku vēṟāṉatu; tāṉē aṟivataṉṟi, uṇartta uṇarvatu; uṭaṟ kāraṇaṅkaḷ kūṭi iruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṟku vēṟāy niṉṟu aṟivatu; paṇṭik kāla mutalē āṇavamalattāl kaṭṭuṇṭu kiṭappatu; puṟa - akakkaruvikaḷum uyirum, aracum amiccum pōlvaṉa. ak karuvikaḷ uyirukkut tuṇi ceyvaṉa. ivi yāvum toḻiṟpaṭum kālam uyirukku naṉavu nili eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ cila kuṟinta kālam uyirukkuk kaṉavu nili; mēlum cila kuṟinta nili uṟakka nili eṉappaṭum. ivi yāvum nīṅki uyir taṉṉiliyil niṉṟapōtu pēruṟakka nili eṉappaṭum. uyiriṉ akaṅkāramum oṭuṅkiya nili uyirppu aṭakkam eṉappaṭum.
karuvikaḷ aṟivaṟṟaṉa ātaliṉ tammiyum aṟiyā; tammic celuttum uyiriṉiyum aṟiyā. avipōla uyirkaḷum tammi yaṟiyā; tammic celuttum iṟivaṉiyum aṟiyā.
cattu eṉṟum kēṭiṉṟi viḷaṅkum poruḷ. eṉavē iṟi oṉṟē avvilakkaṇattukku ilakkiyamākum. āyiṉum, civa cittāntattil mupporuḷkaḷākiya iṟi, uyir, ulakam (pati, pacu, pācam) eṉpaṉa eṉṟum uḷḷa poruḷkaḷāka oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭavi. ākavē, ivi mūṉṟum cattēyākum. uyirum ulakamum vikāram aṭivatāl acattātal peṟappaṭum. im mūṉṟiṉiyum vēṟu pirittu aṟitaṟku ivi muṟiyē civa- cittu, cata- cattu, caṭa- cattu eṉṟu pirikkappaṭum.
cārntataṉ vaṇṇamātal uyiriṉ ciṟappilakkaṇamākum. aḵtu acattākiya ulakattiyum, cattākiya paramporuḷiyum aṟiyavallatu; acatti viṭṭuc cattip paṟṟakkūṭiyatu. uyirkaḷiṉ tavattāl iṟivaṉ kuru vaṭivil vantu, pakkuvam aṭivataṟku ñāṉatti uṇarttit taṉpāl avarkaḷic cērppaṉ. ñāṉam peṟṟavar ainteḻuttu ōti ñāṉa niliyik kāppar.
ivvāṟu ñāṉattip pēṇum uyirkaḷ, iṟivaṉ tammuṭaṉ ōṟṟittu niṟṟalāl pācam nīṅkappeṟum. iṟivaṉ uyirkaḷukkut tuṇiyāka niṉṟu civappēṟu allatu mutti niliyik kāṭṭuvāṉ; ataṉik kāṇumpaṭi utaviyum ceyvāṉ. cīvaṉ muktarkaḷ malanīkkak karuttuṭiyavarāy, aṭiyār iṇakkam uṭiyavarāyc civa vēṭattiyum civaṉ kōviliyum vaḻipaṭum niyamamum uṭiyavarāy niṟpar. civañāṉapōtam paṉṉiraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa poruḷ ituvēyākum.

12. ilakkiyak kali
ilakkiyam
coṟkaḷiyum vākkiyaṅkaḷiyum uṇṭākkip pāṭavum pēcavum aṟinta makkaḷ, eḻuttu muṟiyik kaṇṭaṟinta piṉpu, tām atuvariyil vāḻiyaṭi vāḻiyāka aṟintu vittirunta camutāyak karuttukaḷiyum, camayak karuttukaḷiyum pākkaḷil vaṭittaṉar. kālam cellac cella, aṟivu vaḷara vaḷara, moḻi vaḷam peṟṟatu. moḻiyil valla aṟiñarkaḷ makkaḷ paṭitta ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum pēccu moḻiyiyum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, moḻikku ilakkaṇam vakuttaṉar. piṉ vanta pulavarkaḷ anta ilakkaṇa amitikkēṟpat tamiḻar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum nākarikattiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum kuṟikkum ceyyuṭkaḷic ceytaṉar.
ceyyuḷ 
ceyyuḷ eṉpatu ēṟṟa cila coṟkaḷāl pala poruḷ kaḷi uḷḷaṭakkip poruttamāṉa muṟiyil ceyyappaṭuvatu.
 	 cilvaki eḻuttil palvakip poruḷic
cevvaṉ āṭiyil ceṟittiṉitu viḷakkit
tiṭpa nuṭpam ciṟantaṉa cūttiram
eṉpatu naṉṉūl nūṟpā. ceyyuḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum ēṟattāḻa ituvē eṉṉalām.
	 palvakit tātuviṉ uyirkkuṭal pōlpala
collāl poruṭkiṭa ṉāka vuṇarviṉil
vallōr aṇipeṟac ceyvaṉa ceyyuḷ
eṉpatum kāṇka.
ilakkiyam eṉpatu makkaḷukkellām potuvākap payaṉpaṭum poruḷi uṭiyatāy iruttal vēṇṭum; attuṭaṉ iṉiya vakiyil collappaṭuvatāy iruttal vēṇṭum. coṟkaṭkuc ciṟanta iṉpam payattalik kuṟik kōḷākap peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum; makkaḷuṭiya vāḻkki nikaḻccikaḷi eṭuttukkūṟum ceyyuṭkaḷō, nūlkaḷō, paṭippōratu uḷḷattik koḷḷi koṇṭu, avaratu vāḻkkiyiyum vaḷappaṭutta vallaṉavātal vēṇṭum. iviyē ilakkiyam eṉappaṭum. (Literature is thus fundamentally an expression of Life through the medium of Language.- An Introduction to the study of Literature, Hudson, p. 11.) apimaṉyu kollappaṭṭa aṉṟiravu avaṉi niṉittu avaṉ tantiyāṉa aruccuṉaṉ pulampum pakutiyip paṭippavar, tāmum kaṇṇīrviṭṭu aḻutalip pārkkiṉṟōm. iṟappu ellōrkkum potu. iṟantatu kuṟittu pulamputalum ellōrkkum potu. eṉavē, ip pakuti aṉivar uḷḷaṅkaḷiyum īrkkiṉṟatu.
vāḻkki nikaḻccikaḷi evarum kūṟalām. āyiṉ, avaṟṟic cātāraṇa maṉitaṉ kūṟuvataṟkum ilakkaṇa ilakkiyam kaṟṟuḷḷa pulavaṉ kūṟuvataṟkum niṟinta vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. cātāraṇa maṉitaṉ kūṟuvatu elumpuk kūṭu pōṉṟatu. kaṟṟuvalla pulavaṉ kūṟuvatu uyiruḷḷa uṭal pōṉṟatu. nāppulami vāynta pulavar kalittiṟam poruntap poruḷi pukaṭṭuvatāl maṭṭumē nam uṇarvu kavarappaṭukiṉṟatu. iṉṉa poruḷi, iṉṉiṉṉa coṟkaḷāl, iṉṉiṉṉavāṟu, iṉṉiṉṉa kālattil colla vēṇṭum eṉpati ap perumakkaḷ naṉkaṟivārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ceyyum ceyyuṭkaḷō nūlkaḷō kaṟpavarkaḷ uḷḷattip piṇikkavallavi. ittakiya āṟṟalāl ilakkiyam kali ākiṉṟatu.
ivvāṟu ciṟanta muṟiyil ākkappeṟṟa nūlkaḷ taliyāya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟu aṟiñarāl karutappeṟum. ap perumakkaḷatu ceyyuṭ ciṟappiṉi naṉku uṇarnta kampar perumāṉ piṉvarumāṟu pārāṭṭutal kāṇka:
	 puviyiṉuk kaṇiyāy āṉṟa poruḷ tantu pulattiṟ ṟāki
aviyakat tuṟikaḷ tāṅki aintiṇi neṟiya ḷavic
caviyuṟat teḷintu taṇṇeṉ ṟoḻukkamum taḻuvic cāṉṟōr
kaviyeṉak kiṭanta kōtā variyiṉi vīrar kaṇṭār.
ilakkiyat tōṟṟam
oru camutāyattil ilakkiyam veḷippaṭuvaṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ yāvi?
oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar kalantu vāḻutal makkaḷ iyalpu. aṅṅaṉam makkaḷ oṉṟukūṭi vāḻntāltāṉ poruḷ uṇarcciyum aṟivu vaḷarcciyum uṇṭākum. makkaḷ tam uṇarccikaḷiyum eṇṇaṅkaḷiyum piṟarkku eṭuttuk kāṭṭa viḻital iyalpākum; iṅṅaṉam eṭuttukkāṭṭi kēṭpavar avaṟṟi aṟiyumpoḻutu tammip pōlavē uṇartalum eṇṇutalum ceytu ceytu makiḻvatip pārppatil iṉpam tōṉṟukiṟataṉṟō? ivvāṟu makkaḷ tammi veḷippaṭuttu vataṟku virumpum viruppa mikuti ilakkiyat tōṟṟattiṟku oru kāraṇamākum. im muṟiyil eḻuntaviyē akapporuḷ, puṟapporuḷ paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷ eṉṉalām.
makkaḷ tam karuttukaḷip piṟarukku eṭuttuk kūṟuvatōṭu, piṟar eṇṇaṅkaḷiyum uṇarccikaḷiyum aṟintu koḷvatil viruppam kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar; avaratu vāḻkki nikaḻccikaḷik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar allatu tuṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar; avaṟṟip piṟarukku eṭuttukkūṟi avaṟṟiṉ paṭippiṉiyi uṇarttukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟāl avarkkut tammi yoḻinta piṟariṭattuḷḷa paṟṟu naṉku pulaṉākum. intap paṟṟē cilappatikāram mutaliya peruṅkāviyaṅkaḷ eḻutak kāraṇamāy amintatu. muṟṟum tuṟanta iḷaṅkō aṭikaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ kāraṇamākavē cilappatikāratti yāttaṉar eṉpatu,
	 ariciyal piḻittōrkku aṟaṅkūṟ ṟāvatūum
uricāl pattiṉikku uyarntōr ēttalum
ūḻviṉi yuṟuttuvan tūṭṭumeṉ patūum
cūḻviṉic cilampu kāraṇa mākac
cilappati kāram eṉṉum peyarāl
nāṭṭutu miyāmōr pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷ
eṉṟu kūṟiyatik koṇṭu uṇaralām. cintāmaṇi, maṇimēkali, periyapurāṇam, irāmāyaṇam mutaliya kati nūlkaḷ immuṟi paṟṟiyē ceyyappeṟṟavi.
makkaḷ, tuṉpuṟum ivvulaka vāḻkkiyik kaṇṭu kaṇṭu, iṉpam kāṇum muṟiyil īṭupaṭutalum ilakkiyat tōṟṟattiṟku oru kāraṇamākum. itaṉāl tāṉ, illatu- iṉiyatu- nallatu- puṉinturi eṉap pulavarāl nāṭṭap peṟṟa oḻukkam nāṉkeṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. kōvi, kalampakam pōṉṟa puṉinturi nūlkaḷ immuṟi paṟṟi eḻuntaviyēyākum.
uḷḷattit tiṟappataṟkum, atit tiruttuvataṟkum, purintukoṇṭu paṭippatic cerippataṟkum, tavaṟukaḷip pōkkum vaṉmi uṇṭākkuvataṟkum, nallavaṟṟi mēṟ koḷvataṟkum, piṟarkku eṭutturippataṟkum, piṟari nalvaḻipaṭuttutaṟkum ilakkiyam payaṉpaṭal vēṇṭum. inniliyiṟṟāṉ aḵtu ilakkiyam eṉappaṭum, eṉpatu kārṭiṉal niyūmaṉ eṉpavaratu karuttākum.
ivvāṟu ilakkiyam pala kāraṇaṅkaḷik koṇṭu tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivaṟṟuḷ oḻuṅku, variyaṟi, aḻaku, payaṉ ākiyavi niṟintuḷḷa ilakkiya nūlkaḷē paṭippōrkku iṉpa uṇarcciyi uṇṭākkum. aviyē camutāyattiṉ aḻiyāta celvaṅkaḷākum. nam tamiḻ nūlkaḷuṭ pala ittakiya aḻiyāta celvaṅkaḷākum. avaṟṟuḷ taliciṟantavi caṅka nūlkaḷ; uḷḷati uḷḷavāṟu urippavi.
toki nūlkaḷ
puṟanāṉūṟu, akanāṉūṟu, naṟṟiṇi, kuṟuntoki, aiṅkuṟu nūṟu, patiṟṟup pattu, paripāṭal, kalittoki eṉpavi caṅkakālat toki nūlkaḷ eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ paripāṭal eṉpatu, murukaṉ, tirumāl, viyi paṟṟip palar pāṭiya pākkaḷik koṇṭatu. ovvoru pāṭṭiṟkum cantam uṇṭu. tirumāl, murukaṉ ākiya kaṭavuḷar alaṅkārac ciṟappum, ak kaṭavuḷar ceyta vīrac ceyalkaḷum, piṟavum aṭiyārkku aruḷum tiṟamum poruḷpotinta coṟkaḷāl pēcap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. viyi yāṟṟil putuveḷḷam varutal, av veḷḷam vayalkaḷ mutaliya pala iṭaṅkaḷilum paravutal, avaṟṟāl uḻavar mutaliyōr makiḻvuṟutal, nakara makkaḷ putunīrāṭap pōtal, avartam nilikaḷ, avarkaḷ nīrāṭutal, viyip perukkiṟku vaṇakkam terivikkum muṟi, āṟṟaṅkariyil nikaḻum āṭal pāṭal nikaḻccikaḷ mutaliya viyi paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷ aḻakoḻuka eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṇṭip pulavar perumakkaḷ kaṭavuḷartam alaṅkāra vakikaḷiyum, potumakkaḷ pakti niṟinta uḷḷattōṭu kōvilukku varutaliyum, ak kaṭavuḷari vaḻipaṭutaliyum, ivvāṟē viyiyil putuveḷḷam varutaliyum, potumakkaḷ makiḻcciyōṭu nīrāṭi āṭal pāṭalkaḷ nikaḻttu taliyum tam viḻiyārak kaṇṭu kaṇṭu makiḻntavar; ak kāṭcikaḷi niṉiviṟ koṇṭum, tām paṭitta nūlkaḷiṉ tuṇiyik koṇṭum, tamatu kaṟpaṉit tiṟaṉum pulamit tiṟaṉum pulappaṭa ip pāṭalkaḷip pāṭiṉar. eṉavē, ip pāṭalkaḷ ilakkiyak kalivaḷam ceṟintu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
akapporuḷ nūlkaḷ
akanāṉūṟu, naṟṟiṇi, kuṟuntoki, aiṅkuṟunūṟu eṉpavi, illaṟam nallaṟamāka naṭattakkūṭiya uḷḷam oṉṟupaṭṭa talimakkaḷatu aka vāḻvic cittarittuk kūṟuvaṉa. ivvaka vāḻvu kuṟiñci, pāli, mulli, marutam, neytal eṉṉum aintu oḻukkaṅkaḷāka nikaḻkiṉṟatu eṉṟu, pulavarkaḷ ivaṟṟukku kuṟiñci mutaliya aintu tiṇikaḷi oppiṭṭup pāṭiyuḷḷa tiṟam ciṟanta kalit tiṟaṉit taṉṉakattē koṇṭuḷḷatākum. ovvoru nilattiṟkum uriya maram ceṭi koṭikaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, perum poḻutu, ciṟu poḻutu, paṟi, paṇ, toḻilkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi muṟip paṭutti, avvoḻukkatti viḷakkum pāṭalkaḷ avvat tiṇikkuriya mutaṟporuḷ, karupporuḷ, uripporuḷ eṉpavi poruntap pāṭiyuḷḷa tiṟaṉik kalittiṟaṉ eṉṟu collalām. caṅkap pulavar pāṭalkaḷip paṭittup paṭittu aṟintu makiḻpavarē avartam kalittiṟaṉi naṉku uṇara muṭiyum.
kalittokiyil ciṟanta kalimakkaḷatu akavoḻukkamum iḻintōr akavoḻukkamum kalantu pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iḻintōr paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷil iḻintōrkkuriya paṇpu kaḷiyum, avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum uvamikaḷiyum coṟṟoṭar kaḷiyum karuttukkaḷiyum vittup pāṭiyiruttal, caṅkap pulavartam kalittiṟaṉiyum, paranta ulaka aṟiviyum naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟatu. ilakkiyak kalikku ivvakap poruḷ nūlkaḷ ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭu eṉṉalām.
tamiḻaṉ, pōri veṭci mutaliya pala tiṇikaḷākap pirittāṉ; ovvoru tiṇikkum pala tuṟikaḷi amittāṉ. ovvoru tuṟiyum pōr muyaṟciyiṉ oru paṭiyākum. iṅṅaṉam pōr muṟikaḷukku ilakkaṇam vakutta tamiḻaṉ, pōrukku appāṟpaṭṭa ceyalkaḷukkum pala pakutikaḷi vakuttāṉ. ivi aṉittiyum tolkāppiyam- puṟattiṇi iyalilum, puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māli yilum virivākak kāṇalām. it tiṇikaḷukkum, tuṟi kaḷukkum uriya pāṭalkaḷip puṟanāṉūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu eṉṉum iraṇṭu nūlkaḷilum paṭittu makiḻalām. ovvoru pāṭṭiṉ aṭiyilum, iḵtu iṉṉa tiṇi- iṉṉa tuṟi eṉṟu paṇṭip pulavarkaḷ eḻuti vittuḷḷārkaḷ. tiṇiyum tuṟiyum paṟṟip pēcum ilakkaṇattiṟkuriya eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ovvoru pāṭalum amintiruppatu paṭittu uṇarntu makiḻaṟ kuriyatu. mikac ciṟanta kaliyuṇarvu uṭiya pulavar perumakkaḷāltām ittakiya pāṭalkaḷip pāṭutal iyalum.
tirukkuṟaḷ 
tirukkuṟaḷ aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam eṉṟa mūṉṟu poruḷkaḷiyum virittup pēcuvatu; iṭiyiṭiyē vīṭu pēṟṟiyum kuṟippatu; vāḻkkikkut tēviyāṉa ovvoru viḻumiya karuttiyum kuṟaṭpāvil terivippatu. kuṟaṭpā iraṇṭu aṭikaḷik koṇṭatu; ēḻu cīrkaḷi uṭiyatu. oru ciṟanta karutti ēḻu cīrkaḷāl oṉṟē mukkāl aṭiyil amittal eḷitāṉa ceyalā? oru kuṟaḷil oru colli nīkki nām vēṟoru colli amittāl, ak kuṟaḷiṉ poruḷ amitiyum collamitiyum keṭṭu viṭutal kāṇkiṟōm. iṅṅaṉam piṟar ki vikka muṭiyātapaṭi, ciṟanta coṟkaḷi amittu avaṟṟāl viḻumiya karutti viḷakkuvatu ciṟanta kaliyāṟṟal aṉṟō? ittakiya kali āṟṟalit tirukkuṟaḷil kaṇṭu kaṇṭu cuvikkalām.
cilappatikāram
cilappatikāram tamiḻk kāviyaṅkaḷuḷ taliciṟantatu eṉṟu aṉivarum pōṟṟutaṟku, ataṉkaṇ amintuḷḷa kali āṟṟalē ciṟanta kāraṇamākum. atu maṉita vāḻvic cittarittuk kūṟum pērilakkiyam; maṉitaṉuṭiya nalliyalpu kaḷiyum, tīya iyalpukaḷiyum paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭuvatu; oṉpatu cuvikaḷiyum tiṟampaṭa eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu; tamiḻakattiṉ aivaki nilaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ iyalpu kaḷiyum virittuk kūṟuvatu; moḻippaṟṟu - iṉap paṟṟu- nāṭṭuppaṟṟu eṉpavaṟṟi uyir nāṭiyākak koṇṭu iruppatu; viruppu veṟuppu iṉṟic cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar mūvariyum oṉṟu pōlavē pārāṭṭuvatu. ittakiya paṇpukaḷōṭu viḻumiya coṟkaḷāl yākkappeṟṟa akavaṟ pākkaḷiyum veṇpākkaḷiyum taṉṉakattē peṟṟiruppatu; pala karuttukkaḷ alimōtum uḷḷattiṟkuc ciṟanta aṟivuriyi iṟutiyil aḷittu niṟpatu. ittakiya uyarnta paṇpukaḷāl cilappatikāram eṉṉum kāviyam taliciṟanta ilakkiyak kali nūlāyk kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.
muṭivuri
ivvāṟē piṉtōṉṟiya nūlkaḷāṉa cintāmaṇi, periya purāṇam, kampa rāmāyaṇam eṉpaṉa ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṉalām. civat tirumuṟikaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum, āḻvār aruṭpāṭalkaḷum camaya ilakkiyam; patiṉāṉku cāttiraṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa tattuva ilakkiyam. paraṇi, kalampakam, ulā, antāti, piḷḷittamiḻ, kuṟam mutaliya ciṟu nūlkaḷ pulavartam ilakkiyak kaliyāṟṟali naṉmuṟiyil veḷip paṭuttuvaṉavākum. iṅṅaṉamē ilakkiyap paṇpukaḷ aminta ik kālak kati nūlkaḷ kavitikaḷ, nāṭaka nūlkaḷ mutaliyaṉavum ilakkiya araṅkil iṭampeṟum. ivaṟṟiyellām ilakkiyak kali uṇarvōṭu paṭittu iṉpuṟutalē tamiḻar kaṭamiyākum.
